26b2(rgyud): mthu yis ni//mnyam med ye shes yang dag bshad //de bas kun mkhyen thob bya'i phyir /bu yis blo gros 'di bgyis shig//bla ma mchod dang de bzhin byams //sangs rgyas la gus 'gro la rtse rtsa ba'i
26b3(rgyud): ltung ba yongs spangs shing//sbom po'i ltung ba rnam par spangs//sems can rnams ni sten par bya//theg pa dman pa 'dod mi bya//ma sbal ba rnams sgral bar bgyi//ma grol ba rnams gral bar
26b4(rgyud): bgyi//'khor ba'i sdug sngal bkrugs pa yis //sems can dbugs ni nga byung bar gyis//slab pa de dag yang dag ldan//slob ma rnaMs ni lhags gnas shing//go cha ci bzhin khrus dang ni/
26b5(rgyud): bsas byas so shing sbyin par bya//srad bu dmar pos khyad par du /dpung pa bcis te rtag tu bsrung//yi ge hri'i sngags bzlas pa'i //ku sha de la rab tu sbyin //rmi lam dge dang mi dge rtag /
26b6(rgyud): sku gsung thugs kyi sdom pa sbyin //yon tan lhan cig mnyam ldan pa'i//me tog sil ma rab bzung nas//der ni gdong g.yogs cing nas su //dkyil 'khor du ni rab tu bzhug //skye ba sngon 
26b7(rgyud): rdzogs sdig pa rnams dkyil 'khor mthong bas byang bar 'gyur//kye khyod su zhes dri byas shing//bdag ni bskal bzangs zhes kyang smra//dam tshig chu dang gna' skeg dang//me tog dkyil 'khor dor
26b8(rgyud): ba ba ste//gang dang gang du me tog babs //de dang de'i rigs su 'gyur //chu dang gcod pan rdo rje dang //bril bu dang ni mi dbang bskur//de bzhin gshegs mnga' bdag nyid dbang //rtul zhugs dang ni lung
27a1(rgyud): @#//bstan nyid//rjes gnang ni dbus dbyung dang phyir mi ldog pa 'ang sbyin pa ste//gdugs dang bdan dang rig stsogs//rdo rje dril rtsogs yang dag ldan//slob dpon dbang skur yang dag rdzog+sa//
27a2(rgyud): bum pa las ni byung ba'o//gnyis pa gsang ba mchog yin te//gsum pa shes rab ye shes yin//bzhi pa de ltar yang de bzhin //dbang skur 'di dag yang dag ldan//de ni dam tshig can
27a3(rgyud): zhes bya//gsang ba mchog gi dkyil 'khor du//mchog tu rab zhugs mthong bas na//deng khyod sdig pa thams cad las//rnam grol bzang por gyur pas gnas //sangs rgyas kun gyis
27a4(rgyud): mnyam gsungs pas//rjes gnang mchog tu dbugs dbyung ba dngos grub dam tshig sdom pa ni//'di dag rtag tu bsrung bar gyis//slob ma dad pa'i stobs kyis ni //bla ma'i
27a5(rgyud): zhabs la phyag 'tshal nas//gtso bos ji ltar bka' stsal pa/de ltar bdag gis thams cad bgyi//de nas de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs pa yi//sna tshogs brgyan dang
27a6(rgyud): na bza' sogs /khyad par du ni rang gi lus //bla ma la ni yon du dbul//dbang skur gtso bo thob pas na/bya ba byas pa'i blo can gyis //gzhan yang rab dgags 'di skad du //smra ba'i 'jug ni
27a7(rgyud): rdzogs par bya//deng dus bdag tshe 'bras bu yang //bdag gi gson pa 'bras bur bcas //de ring sangs rgyas rigs su skyes//sangs rgyas sras por bdag gyur to//sbyin sreg gis ni
27a8(rgyud): rdzogs bya zhing //dge 'dun la ni bston mo drang //de nas tshogs kyi 'khor lo bya//nad pa 'am mgon med rnams la byin//phyi nas man ngag ci bzhin du //dam tshig spyod pa la brtson pas//
27b1(rgyud): snod du byas pa'i rgyun gyis ni//'khor lo la sogs sgom pa'i rim//yang dag man ngag phun tshogs pas//dngos grub 'gyur gyis gzhan du min //'di ni dbang bskur ba'i rim pa phye ba ste//
27b2(rgyud): bcwo brgyad pa'o////de nas 'chi bar nges pa'i mtshan nyid mchog tu bshad par bya//rang gi lus las phyi rol gyi //ltas ni mkhas pas rtag par bya//rkang 'thil dag tu phug snyam
27b3(rgyud): dang //gang tshe lte bar phug snyam 'gyur //de tshe nyin zhag gsum pha rol /lnga'i gong du 'gro bar 'gyur//gshang dang gci ba'i dus dag tu//dus mtshungs srid pa 'byung 'gyur na//lo cig na dus
27b4(rgyud): de nyid kyis //dus tshod la ni 'chi zhing rlag //mtshan gnyis rnam par sbyor ba yis//bar dang tha mar srid pa ni //dus mtshungs 'byung na de'i tshe//zla ba gcig na nges par 'chi//snying
27b5(rgyud): kha lkog ma'i dbus dag tu //dus mtshungs gal te zug zer 'gyur //ci ste chos la mi rten na//zla ba phye dang gnyis na 'chi//gal te mig g.yon 'bras bu yi//gzugs
27b6(rgyud): snyan me long la ma mthong //ci ste zlog par mi byed na//zhag bdun na ni nges par 'chi//rna rtsa dang ni smin ma'i dbus //spyi bo'i rtse mor zug pa ste//bzhi po mtshungs par zug 'gyur na/
27b7(rgyud): de tshe 'phral du 'chi bar 'gyur//blo bur du ni 'tsho 'gyur zhing //ring dang khro dang 'jigs par 'gyur 'khrugs//gal te chos la ma rten gang //de ni lo gcig dag na 'chi//yar ngo'i ni chos
27b8(rgyud): gcig na//gal te khu ba nag po 'thon//de tshe zla ba drug na 'chi//dmar po'i nad kyis thebs par bston//mig las rtag tu 'dzag pa dang gzugs mthong ba yang rnaM 'phrul ba//me long dang
28a1(rgyud): @#//ni tshur gang zhig//rag+si grib ma mthong dang//mtshan mo'i tshe na dbang po'i gzhu//nyin par skar ma'i dkyil 'khor mthong//srin med pas ni lho phyogs su//rten pa'i klog 'gyur mthong ba
28a2(rgyud): dang//nyin par grib ma'i lam dang ni//de bzhin skar mda' ltung ba mthong //ngang pa bya rog rma bya ni//gcig tu 'dres pas mthong ba dang//zla ba gnyis dang nyi ma gnyis//de bzhin rang gi mog
28a3(rgyud): bor 'bar//shing rtse ri'i rtse la ni//dri za'i grong khyer mthong ba dang//yi dgas sha za 'jigasu rung //gzhan dag kyang ni mthong ba dang//blo bur du ni rab 'dar dang //yang dag dang yang du rgyal ba
28a4(rgyud): dang //de yis gcig gcig mthong 'gyur na 'ang//zla ba gcig gis 'chi bar 'gyur//zla bar rib med pa dang//nyi ma 'od dang bral ba dang//mtshan mo nyi ma nyin b+har ni//zla ba de bzhin rang
28a5(rgyud): mig 'bar//skar ma ri rab dag gi tshad//rgya mtsho chu bo lta bu dang///bu ba dang ni gshang gci rnams//dus mtshungs par ni 'bab 'gyur na//gal te chos la mi rten na//zla ba phyed
28a6(rgyud): na 'chi bar 'gyur//de yang nyin mo grib ma ni//skye bo'i gzugs can lta bya ste//de yi mgo bo ma mthong na//log cig gi ni nang du 'chi/lag pa yon pa mthong na//bu dang ma gnyis rlag par
28a7(rgyud): 'gyur//lag pa g.yas pa ma mthong na//pha dang bu rtsogs tshogs chen 'chi//gcin ni rgyun lngar 'gyur ba dang //g.yon nas 'khor zhing nga dang/skur ba lasogs gcin 'byung ba//zla drug
28a8(rgyud): nang du 'chi bar 'gyur//gang zhig gi ni rmi lam mthar//bye ma 'am thal ba'i phung po 'am//lha gang 'am ni mchod sdong 'dzegs//de ni sngon bzhin 'chi bar 'gyur//gal te spre'u bong la zhon//
28b1(rgyud): grog mkhar phyag 'dar phung po la //mngon 'dzegs lho phyogs 'gro ba ni//gang gis rmi lam mthar mthong ba//bud med gang zhig gos nag cing //mdog nag mi ni 'dod spyod pa//de ni dus mtshan
28b2(rgyud): yin zhes bya//gshin rje'i lto bar 'gro bar 'gyur //bya rog gce sbyang khyi dang wa//dred dang yi dgas sha za yis//za bar rmi lam mthong na ni//lo gcig nas ni nges par 'chi//gos ni dmar
28b3(rgyud): pos g.yogs pa dang//phre ba dmar pos brgyan pa dang//til mar gyis skus rmi lam mthong//zla ba drug na gson mi 'gyur//man ngag ji bzhin ldan pa yis //'chi ba slu bar 'gyur ba ste//
28b4(rgyud): de nyid des ni 'chi las rgyal//chos kyis 'chi las rgyal ba ste//rdzogs ba'i byang chub rim bsgrub pas//des ni chos ni mchog tu bsam//gzhan yang sgom ba'i khyad par ni //mdzes pas
28b5(rgyud): yang dag bshad par bya //dbugs ni 'chad cing chad pa dang//sna tshogs ltas ni yang dag mthong //mthor dang dga' bI sbyor ba yis //lus kyi dkyil 'khor sbyar bar bya//'chi ba'i dus
28b6(rgyud): la yang dag bab //'pho ba'i rnal 'byor mchog bya ste//bu ga dgur ni son pI rtsa //pa'i kas ni gang byar bya//re rtsa kas ni dbugs gnan bya//kum ba kas ni sngams byas te//bu ga'i sgo ni sbyang
28b7(rgyud): par bya//rab tu zhi bas zhi bar 'gyur //rnam shes 'phrog pa'i sbyor ba dang //gzhan yang pha rol bgrod pa la //a li ki li mnyam ldan par //mkhas yis ni sbyar bar bya//snying khar yig
28b8(rgyud): hU~M sbyar te//yi ge phye dang gnyis po ni //steng dang 'og tu bzhag par bya//de'i 'og gi char rlung gi//sa bon 'og tu ltas pa'o//rlung gi sa bon gnyis byas te//rnal 'byor ldan pas kha sbyar bya//
29a1(rgyud): @#//nyi shu rtsa gcig rim pa yis //yi ge phyed dang gnyis sngags rjod rnam shes zhon pa rlung yin te//gang dang gang du 'gro//ba yis /rlung gi sgo bo ni bsam byas nas//grol ba'i dngos grub ster byed pa'o//
29a2(rgyud): mchog dang tha ma'i dbye ba ni//bshad kyis gsang ba'i bdag po nyon//lte bas 'dod khams bde 'gro ste//thig las ni gzugs can lus//steng nas gzugs med khamasu ste//de ni bde bar 'gro 
29a3(rgyud): byed pa'o//sna las gnod sbyin rnamasu 'gyur//rna ba las ni mi 'am ci//lha mo gal te mig las ni//mi'i rgyal por 'gyur ba yin//kha'i sgo nas yi dgas rnams //gci las de bzhin dud 'gro'o
29a4(rgyud): bshad pa las ni dmyal bar 'gro//grol ba las ni gzhan du 'gro //dus la bab na 'pho bya'i //dus min lha rnams bsod par 'gyur//lha rnams bsad pa tsam gyis ni//mi ni dmyal
29a5(rgyud): bar 'chod par//des na mkhas pas 'chi ba yi//mtshan ma yang dag shes par bya//'di ni 'chi ba'i ltas dang 'pho ba'i rnal 'byor rim pa phye ba ste bcu dgu pa'o////
29a6(rgyud): de nas de phyir dus bzhi'i //tshad ni yang dag rab bshad bya//gang zhig rnam par shes tsam gyis //skal ba myur du shes par 'gyur pa'o//nyi khri dang ni brgya stong dang //'bum phrag gcig bdun 
29a7(rgyud): 'bum ste//de dag rdzogs ldan ni //bskal pa zhes ni grags par bshad //rdzogs ldan gsuM ldan gnyis mtshamasu //stong phrag drug ni shes par bya//drug khri dang ni bzhi stong dang //'bum phrag
29a8(rgyud): brgyad ni gnyis ldan te//bstan bcos kun las sdus nas ni//gnyis ldan bskal par yang dag grags //gnyis ldan rtsod pa'i bar 'tshamasu //de bzhin stong phrag bzhi yin te//sum
29b1(rgyud): khri dang ni nyis stong dang //bzhi 'bum rtsod dus shes par bya//rtsod dus rdzogs ldan gnyis 'tshamasu//stong phrag dag ni bzhi dang ldan//de rnams dus shes grags pa ste//'grub par 'dir ni the 
29b2(rgyud): tsom med//gling bzhi pa'i dngos po ni//mgon po yis ni bshad du gsol//shar gyi lus 'phags sgra mi snyan//de bzhin nub kyi ba lad spyod//gling ni gsum po de dag rnams//'bras bu 
29b3(rgyud): sar pa yang dag gnas//'dzam bu'i gling du legs skyes pa//dngos grub sar ni shes par bya//gling gsum por ni skyes pa yis//'bras bu 'byung ba'i bsod nams gnas//'dzam bu gling pas grol 
29b4(rgyud): ba dang //dngos grub nyid ni bsgrub pa dang //de dag sngon gyi sangs rgyas kyis//gsungs pa'i gling du shes par bya//'di ni dus bzhi po nges par bstan pa'i rim pa phye ba ste nyi shu pa'o////
29b5(rgyud): de nas spyod pa pha rol sems mchog ni yang dag rab bshad bya//bsgrub pos dngos grub rgyu yid kyis//gang gis dngos grub mthar 'grub pa//thun mong rnal 'byor rgyud rnaMsu 
29b6(rgyud): gsang ba rnam par ma bshad par//dngos grub rnams las mchog dngos grub//rtul zhugs rnams las mchog rtul zhugs//bla ma dam pa bsnyen bkur nas//bla med rgyun ni mang thos te//
29b7(rgyud): de nyid ci bzhin bla ma bzhin//zhal nas rtag tu thos nas sgrom//nor dang chung ma de bzhin srog//sbyin pa nyid du btang bar bya//mdud pa 'di gsum las grol ba//rtag tu spyod pa spyad par
29b8(rgyud): 'gyur//spro ba chen pos rig pa zlas//de bzhin bden pa spra la dga'//dam bcas pa la rab gnas te//ci ltar dad pas sngon du rtsam//'dod dang khro bas 'jigs pa dang //sred dang rti
30a1(rgyud): @//mug nga rgyal spangs//slob ma rtag dang de bzhin bshad//gzhung rnams sdu ba rtag tu yang//gtsang dang mi rtag byed dang//bdung dang mi tung rtag mi bya//smad dang bstod la khro ba dang //mngon
30a2(rgyud): pa'i nga rgyal rnam par spangs//rtag tu mi chags dga' med par//thas cad la ni mnyam par gnas//sbyin sreg med cing mchod pa med//phreng ba med cing bzlas pa med//nyi ma bza' dang skar ma
30a3(rgyud): dang//tshes grangs dag kyang rtag mi bya//bdag bzhan bdag nyid gzugs kyis ni//the tsom med par gnas par bya//'dod pa med par mi spyod te//kun la 'dod pa cung zad spyad//stag gi pags
30a4(rgyud): pa gos su byas//phyag rgya lngas ni rnam par brgyan//shes rab thabs dag rnal 'byor pas//he ru ka dpal rna bar sgom//kun du bzang po'i spyod pa yis//'di ni bde bar gnas par
30a5(rgyud): bya//grong du mtshan mo gcig gnas shing//grong khyer du ni sngar gnas bya//yid dang rjes mthun sbyor yis//sa stengs dag tu gnas par bya//yang na rlung zhes bya ba yis//sbyor ba bde zhing
30a6(rgyud): dga' ba spyad//rtag tu grogs po med par rgyu//gcig pur yid ni rtse gcig pas//smin ma'i rtul zhugs la rten nas//lo ma rlung gis skyod bzhin 'khyam//dud khrod dang ni mtshan gcig gam//shing
30a7(rgyud): cig gam ni nags tshal lam//ri rtse 'am chu'i 'gram//rgya mtsho chen po'i 'gram yang rung//skyed mos tshal lam ri phug gam//kham bzangs 'am ni khang stong ngam//gzhi mod dang ni sgo mdun
30a8(rgyud): nam//de bzhin rgyal po'i pho brang 'khor//rdol pa'i gnas sam//phyogs lhas sam//gzo bo khang par sbab pas gnas//tshong srid dor ba'i me tog phreng //me tog snying pa ci yang rung//dur
30b1(rgyud): khrod mtshan cig mi tog phreng //de dag gis ni lus rab mchod//rang gi mgrin par me tog phreng //khyad par du tshangs skud dang //skye rags dag kyang bcing bar bya//rkang pa gnyis la rkang brdum dang //smra ba
30b2(rgyud): bzlas pa'i bdag nyid grags//lag pa rkyong ba phyag rgya ste//rnam par me tog rab sbyor bas//rnal 'byor ni ci 'dir gnas//rtog pa thas cad rnam pa ste//bsgrub pos seng ge bzhin du pyad/yang na
30b3(rgyud): g.yo med brdungs bzhugs la//rten nas rnal 'byor spyod pa spyad//bskyed mos tshal lam khyim stong gnas//grong dman ngan pa'i khyim du ni//ci srid nus pa thob de srid//mi smra'i sbyor bas gnas par
30b4(rgyud): bya//ci ldan gnyid log 'gro dang 'dug//sad nam yang na ma sad pa 'am//ci snyed pa tsam za ba dang //mi kres na ni bzang por gnas//gal te slongs mo gnas pa na//lag pa'i snod du za zhing
30b5(rgyud): gnas//rnam par mi rtog rang bzhin gyis//'dir ni 'grub 'gyur the tsom med//gsum po de dag gnas na ni//gang 'dod rtul zhugs la rten nas//cung zad nus pa rab thob pas//
30b6(rgyud): ci ltar 'dod pa'i spyod pa spyad//lus ni sbyin pa byin nas su//phyi nas spyod pa yang dag rtsam//spyod pas sgyu ma'i rnal 'byor pa//nges par dri ma med gyur ba//'di la 'khrul par mi bya ste//sangs
30b7(rgyud): rgyas rdzu 'phrul bsam mi khyab//'di ni spyod pa nges par bstan pa'i rim pa phye ba ste nyi shu gcig pa'o////de nas gzhan yang bshad bya ba//rnal 'byor rgyud kyi nges don dang //rnal 'byor ma'i
30b8(rgyud): rgyud kyi tshad/bshad kyis gsang ba'i bdag po nyon//rnal 'byor rgyud rnams kyi ni tshad/bye ba khrag ni drug nges te//de bzhin rnal 'byor ma rgyud grags//bye ba khrag ni drug cu grangs//
31a1(rgyud): @#//theg chen las gzhan mdo sde rnams //dung phyur phrag brgyad gsungs pa yin//de bzhin pha rol phyin pa'i tshul //sa ya dang ni dung phyur lnga//'di dag thams cad thub dbang po //sku gsum bdag
31a2(rgyud): nyid can gyis gsungs//bde bar gshegs pas bstan pa'o//'dul bya'i gnas lasogs pa kun //nyan thos bdag nyid kyis bstan pa'o//rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud //gsang ba sangs rgyas spyod yul
31a3(rgyud): te//shes rab thabs bdag rnal 'byor gyis//bde ba chen po sgom pa yis chos dang long spyod rdzogs sprul pa'i//sangs rgyas myur du thob bya'i phyir sems can bsod nams dman rnams la//lha'i
31a4(rgyud): bdag nyid can gyis gsungs//de nas gzhan dag rab gnas dang //cho ga pha rol son bshad bya//sngar bshad mtshan nyid dang ldan pa //slob dpon du ni yongasu rtag //zlugs ma 'am mngon
31a5(rgyud): par 'dus byas pa'i //sku gzugs lasogs pa rnams kyi//rab gnas don du sbyang zhing //bla re rgyal mtshan ba dan rnaMs//rgyan cing sa ni yongs su bzung //sku gzugs sam ni glegs paM 'am//
31a6(rgyud): bris sku yongs su rdzogs pa rnams//mdun rol du ni gzhag pa rnams //dkyil 'khor lo namakhar gnas//nyer spyod lngas yang dag mchod //sa ma ya stom sa ma ya sto//eho//lan gsum du ni
31a7(rgyud): brjod par bya//ci ltar rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kun //dga' ldan yang dag bzhugs nas ni//lha mo sgyu 'phrul lhums 'jug ltar //de bzhin 'dir ni bzhugasu gsol//che ge mo zhes bya'i don
31a8(rgyud): du ni //byang chub sems ni yongs 'phel phyir//me tog lasogs 'di zhes te//mgon po 'dir ni rtag bzhugs 'gyur//tshig 'dis de bzhin gshegs pa rnams//lhag par gnas par byas nas
31b1(rgyud): ni//mkhas pa yis ni bstod byas nas//yungs kar kyis ni skor nas su//lan gsum du ni sbyang bar bya//dri zhim ldan pa'i mar gyis byug //ngag 'chal gyis ni khrus kyang byas //slar yang bum
31b2(rgyud): pa so so yis//sku gzugs rnams ni khrus kyang bya//phyi nas namakha' gnas pa yi//dkyil 'khor lo bcom ldan 'das //zlugs ma la sogs rab bzhugs bya//yul chos la sogs ci
31b3(rgyud): bzhin du//las bcu yis ni bya ba bya//spyan ni dbye ba byas nas su//rdo rje spyan ni byin gyis rlabs//de bzhin me tog spos mar me//byug pa zhal zas de bzhin te//de ltar las ni kun byas phyis//
31b4(rgyud): sbyin sreg las ni rdzogs par bya /lha kun zhal ni me yin pas//mkhas pas rab tu gnas par bya//rab gnas yang dag rdzogs dus su //bstod dang bkra shis brjod bya zhing //mi dang
31b5(rgyud): gar sogs sil snyan sgra//ci ltar rnyed pas rab tu mchod//ston po dang ni yon phul bas//skye bo tshim zhing rgyas par bya/rgyas pas skye bo bskal pa bzang //bkra shis zhi bar bcas 'gyur
31b6(rgyud): te//zhi ba'i sbyor ba la spyod pa //sdug bsngal sna tshogs 'tshe bar 'gyur //ci ltar rab tu mi gnas lta//rab tu gnas par bya bar nus//slob ma dad pas gsol 'debs pas//bsod nams
31b7(rgyud): phyir ni byas pa ste//rnam rtog med pa'i rang bzhin gyis//rab gnas lha'i bzhugs pa yis//lha'i 'jig rten skyong la sogs //gang tshe rtag tu gnas gyur pa //mchod dang mchod bya/mnyam ldan
31b8(rgyud): par;;de tshe lha yang yang dag bzhugs //'di ni lha rnams rab tu gnas pa'i rim pa phye ba ste//nyi shuganyis pa'o////de nas me'i la sogs pa'i//mtshan nyid yang dag bshad par bya//
32a1(rgyud): @#//sa'i sbyang ba byas rjes la //me thab dag ni bya ba ste//sor brgyad pa nas rtsams nas ni //ci srid khru ni stong gi bar //sor brgyad pa ni sgra rnams bsod//sor bcus de bzhin rgyas pa ste//bcu gnyis
32a2(rgyud): sor gyis dbang dang dgug//cu bzhis de bzhin zhi ba'o//thab khung sor ni drug bcu pas//rigs ni mngon par 'phel bar byed //sor ni bco brgyad bdag nyid kyis//yul dang lang rigs 'phel bar
32a3(rgyud): 'gyur //thu kud sor ni nyi shu pas//yams kyi nad ni zhi bar byed //'di dag thab kyi nges pa ste//sreg bya'i tshad kyi rdzas rnams na//las dang rjes su mthun par bya/mkhas pa yis ni shes par
32a4(rgyud): bya//brgya'i tshad gyi cha gsum gyis//cha gnyis zabasu rko byas te//me thab dag ni thams cad kyi //brko ba'i thun mong mtshan nyid do//de'i phyi rol kha ran yang //thab gang gi ni brgyad cha'i//
32a5(rgyud): kha khyer nyid ni thob par bya//kha khyer rbangs kyi phyed tshad nyid//de nyid du nam par bya//ci ltar phyi rol dma' ba'i tshad //de bzhin kha khyer tshad nyid do//de'i phyir ni kha ran yang //phangs
32a6(rgyud): kyi tshad ni ci bzhin bya//thab khung gi ni khyad par du //rdo rje yis ni mtshan par bya//dkar dang ser dang dmar ba dang /de bzhin nag dang ljang ba dang ste//ci ltar las kyi rjes 'gro bas //thab kyi
32a7(rgyud): kha dog mtshan nyid yin //'on kyang las kun byed pa'i thab //zhi ba'i thab kyis khyad par mtshungs//kha khyer pad ma'i rnam pa ste//dma' ba rdo rje'i phreng bas bskor//de'i phyi rol kha khyer ni//
32a8(rgyud): gru bzhi kha khyer rbangs tshad bya//zhi la zlum po'i rnam pa ste//shar ltar kha dog dkar por 'gyur //rgyas pa las ni gru bzhi ste//byang du ltas pas ser po bya//sgra dang mngon par spyod pa las//
32b1(rgyud): zla ba phyed pa nus ltas bya//dbye dang bsang ba'i las ni //zur gsum lhor ni ltas te byas //dbang dang dgug dbye ba gsuM//kha dog dmar po zur gsum pa//gnan dang rmongs pa'i las la ni//bdon 
32b2(rgyud): bral du ni ltas te bya//skrag pa du ba'i mdog lta bu //de bzhin rlung phyogs ltas te bya//rim gyis gdung rtsogs mnan pa'i las//rtag tu me phyogs ltas te bya//lha dang brtan dang rdog rnams ni//las
32b3(rgyud): kyi gzugs kyis 'gyur ba yin//yi ge hU~M gi sbyor ba yis //phyag gnyis pa'i rnam par sgom//skrad cig rnam par gsang sngags brjod//skad cig rnam par lha bdag nyid //rgyus la dga' ba'i seMs
32b4(rgyud): kyis bya//dbang la rjes chags sems kyisu //khro ba'i sems kyis bsad pa ste//rna 'gyur drag po'i sems kyis ni //skrad pa dang ni mngon spyod 'gyur //mchod yon zhabs bsil rtsogs
32b5(rgyud): gzhag bya//de nas me ni spyan rang bya//rang snying chu skyes hU~M yig las//rdo rje sems dpa' rnam par sgom//yig gsum gzhu'i rnam pa la//de dbus ram gyi rnam pa las //kha dog dmar
32b6(rgyud): po 'dzes pa'i zhal//dbyu gu ril ba spyi blug g.yon//g.yas kyi bgrang phreng mi 'jigs sbyin //gsum 'char ral pa'i gcod pan can //brgyan rnams kun gyis brgyan pa ste//sngags pa mkhas pas
32b7(rgyud): lta bar bya//o dza hU~M gi yiges ni //thab khung logs su lta bya ste//gsang rtor 'thor 'thung mchod yon sogs //byin nas thab kyi dbus su gzhag //dam tshig sems dpa' bsams nas ni//
32b8(rgyud): ye shes sems dpa' bzhugs par bya//me tog spos dang mar me dang //de bzhin dri dang zhal zas mchod //lag gnyis pus mo'i nang bzhag ste //dgang gzar dang ni blugs gzar bzung //oM a 
33a1(rgyud): @#//ne ye swAhA//zhes pas dgang blugs bdun nam//gsum sbyin na//de yis me ni tshim 'gyur nas//mi 'jigs pa ni chen po bston//me tog so rtsi na bza' sogs//bstod pas yang ni
33a2(rgyud): mnyes byas te//de nas mthor 'thung sbyin nas ni//me ni yang dag gnas par byas//oM rdo rje bra k+Sha ya swAhA//a shad tha'i 'o/oM badzra la ta ya swAhA//klag sha'i 'o//oM badzra yaM dza ya swAhA//u duM
33a3(rgyud): 'bar ba'i bo//oM badzra ku be re ya swAhA//shing 'o ma can rnams kyi'o//oM sarba pa ta tan badzra ya swAhA//til rnams kyi'o//oM badzra pu shA ya swAhA//'bras ma grus pa rnams kyi'o//oM sarbas
33a4(rgyud): ba de swAhA//zho dang zas kyi'o//oM badzra a yu she swAhA//dur ba'i 'o//oM a sri ta ha na swAhA//ku sha rnams kyi'o//de nas snying ka'i pad ma'i gnas la rang gi lha'i sa bon dang //mtshan
33a5(rgyud): ma yongasu gyur pa las//rdzogs pa'i dkyil 'khor gyi 'khor lo rnam par sgom par byas te//de nas 'khor lo la ye shes kyi 'khor lo bzhug par bya'o//me'i snying kha'i dbusu ni//
33a6(rgyud): skad cig rnam par gnas par bsam//gsang rtor 'thor 'thung rtsogs mchod dang //mchod yon zhal bsil sogs pas mchod//rang lha'i lha sa bon zlas pa yis//the tsom med par sbyin sred
33a7(rgyud): bya//lha rnams so sor phul nas ni//phyi nas ci 'dod sbyin sreg bya//gsum dang bdun dang lhag par ni//ci srid brgya stong bar de bzhin //ji ltar las kyi rjes mthun par //mkhas pa yis ni sbyin
33a8(rgyud): sreg bya//de bzhin zhi ba'i rdzas kun ni //de ltar me lha'i zhal du dbul //yam shing dang ku sha lasogs pa ni 'od kyi dkyil 'khor ro//zhal zas dang 'thor 'thungs lasogs pa ni phyag tu'o//
33b1(rgyud): me tog ni dbul lo//gdug pa ni 'od la'o//byug pa ni thugs kar ro//gsang rtor ni sku la'o//zhabs bsil ni zhabs la'o//mar me mchod yon zhal zas rnams//rim pa ji bzhin bdun du dbul //sngar
33b2(rgyud): bshad chog ji lta bas//dkyil 'khor mchog ni gshegasu+ol//'jig rten sbyin sreg yang dag rdzogs//gal te 'jig rten sangs sbyin sreg nyin mo 'jig rten sbyin sreg ste//de bzhin mtshan mo 'jig rten
33b3(rgyud): 'das//rnal 'byor rnal 'byor ma rnaMs 'dus//bza' dang btung ba'i khyad par rnams//cha 'phyo sgra'i khro bo che//klu dang gar gyi dga' bde ba//rang gi lha'i rnal 'byor gyis//der ni tsa ru'i sbyin sreg bya//
33b4(rgyud): sngon 'dod bya ba gsol btab nas//grub 'gyur de ni the tsam med//oM khyed kyis sems can don kun mdzad//rjesu 'thun pa'i dngos grub brtsol//sangs rgyas yul du gshegs nas kyang ci bde bar
33b5(rgyud): ni bzhesu+ol//gang yang tshangs pa la sogs pa'i//lha 'am 'byung po gang zhig la//dang po sbyin sreg sbyin par bya//oM na ma sa man+ta b+hu d+ha ni//a ku mu ka swa ya shan ting ku ru swAhA//de nas sngags
33b6(rgyud): pa mnyam bzhag cing //mkhas pas mdog dang sgra dang ni//dri dang 'bar ba'i dbyibs rtag cing //de ltar dag dang mi dge'i//lus ni nges par brtson par bya//me ni rtse mo gcig tu 'bar//phun sum tshogs pa
33b7(rgyud): kun byed pa'o//rtse mo gnyis 'bar mi g.yo bar//mtshungs par 'bar na bar mar shes//rtse mo bzhi po'i mnyam du 'am//rgyas shing dngos grub rten par gnas//kun d+ha zla ba lta bu'i mdog //snum zhing
33b8(rgyud): bye du r+ya'i 'od//dri med me ni du ba med//nad med rigs ni 'phel bar byed//zla ba chu shel nor bu'i 'od//kha ba yi ni mdog lta bu//nor bus ba ra ga'i mdog //sdig pa thams cad 'jig byed yin//
34a1(rgyud): @#//me tog ban du ka lta bu //nas kyi me tog gi mdog //bco ma gser gyi mdog lta bu //dbang phyug rgyal srid rab tu bsten//tsam pa pad ma ud par dri //u shil dang ma li ti //shi ta g+ha d+ha'i
34a2(rgyud): dri mtshungs pa //ga bur a ka ru'i dris//dge zhing gnas kyi bdag po mchog //pi bang rgyud mangs rtsa rnga dang //dung dang zangs dung ltar sgra snyan //shin tu zab pa'i sgra snyan pa'i //me mthong bde bar
34a3(rgyud): 'byung bar byed//dpal dbye gdugs dang dung lta bu //gang zhig rtse gsum bum pa'i dbyibs //rgyal mtshan rnga yab rdo rje bzang //g.yung drung rta dang glang po'i dbyibs //sgra med g.yasu 'khyil ba dang /
34a4(rgyud): me lce gcig 'bar don chen ster //'di dag dge ba phun sum tshogs //tshe dang ldan pa med rab ster ba'o//g.yo zhing mdun gyi phyogs su 'bar//rtse gsum du ba'i chags ma
34a5(rgyud): dang //'khor zhing me stag dang bcas pa //rnam par 'phro ba nad med 'phel byed byed//me khang yang yang rab tu 'bar//yang yang rtsub par dgod pa dang //yang yang
34a6(rgyud): g.yon du 'khor ba dang //yang yang sa la me lce reg//thig le nag po sol ba'i mdog //myur du rigs ni zad 'gyur zhing //gang zhig rgyal po'i drung gnas pa'i //sde dang bdag po
34a7(rgyud): 'joms par bston//me med dud pa'i mdog ltar nag //mdog ljang sna tshogs mdog rtsub pa //pa la sha dang 'bru mar mdog//'dis ni don rnams nyams par byed//ro'i dri yi dri ka
34a8(rgyud): ltar ngan//chu skyes srog chags dri dang ldan//ci ste me ni 'di rnams mthong //gtso bo rnam par ltung zhes bston//cig zhes pa'i sgra byung zhing //hur hur gyi ni sgra dang ldan//sim sim
34b1(rgyud): gyi ni sgra dag nyid //rdo rje'i sgras ni don nyams byed//mdung dang ral dri mda' dang sbrub //rnga mo ba 'aglang mgo 'dra gang //'jigs pa'i rnam pa 'di ltar mthong //'di'i mdun na bzod pa gces/
34b2(rgyud): 'di ltar lan gsum brjod nas ni //yon bdag khyim du zhi bde dang //bde legs cha ga'i rim pa bya//de nas gzhan yang sbyin sreg gi //yan lag kun 'byung 'bras rab bshad//
34b3(rgyud): sa dang sa bzhi zhi byas pa'i //thab kyi yan lag yon dag rgyas//mar gyi sbyor ba thams cad byed//ye shes gzi brjid rnam 'phel ba'i//bud shing lhag par dpa' bar byed//ku shas thams cad srung bar
34b4(rgyud): yungs dkar pos zhi byed//'bras kyis rgyas par byed pa ste//til gyis sdig pa 'joms rig bya//so bas nor dang 'bru rnams 'gugs//ma shas stobs ni chen por byed//nas
34b5(rgyud): kyis rlung gi shugs rab ster//dur bas tsho ni 'phel bar byed//dro y+si nad ni 'joms par byed//'o ma sbrang rtsis shes rab 'phel //lag pan gyis ni bde kun ster//mes ni 'dod don dngos grub
34b6(rgyud): ster//rang 'dod ltas ni grol bar rtsol//lhag ma las ni rjes mthun par //zhi rdzogs las bya shes par bya dgang gzar shes rab bzlugs gzar thabs //de gnyis sbyar ba gnyis med sgom//de las
34b7(rgyud): nges 'byung bar gyi rgyun//rgyu dang rgyu'i bdag nyid kyi //ye shes chen po'i bdud rtsir 'dod//me'i las ni rjes mthun par//me ni de yis yang dag mchod//'di ltar sbyin sreg gang byed la//bskal bzangs dngos
34b8(rgyud): grub rab tu ster//'di ni sbyin sreg nges par bstan pa'i rim par phye ba ste nyi shu gsum pa'o////de nas las ni rab 'byam 'byung //dngos grub sna tshogs go 'phang sngags//sna tshogs las
35a1(rgyud): @#//kyi 'byung ba yang //yang dag par ni rab bshad bya//oM na mo sarba sma shwa na bA si nA//ma hA ka la a nu rA tsa yA//tad+ya thA//oM tsa la pra tsa la//la la lA lA yA//de bzhin lam ga swA hA//bsnyen pa/
35a2(rgyud): khri byas te//mtshan phyed na ling ga gzugs te//ci srid 'ongs pa'i bar de srid du bzlas pa byas te//gong bu dgug pa'i//oM tsa mud te//rtum kre shi cid t+yu rdzi te//ha na ha na/pa tsa pa tsa/de ba tad ya a na ya swA hA//
35a3(rgyud): rkang pa g.yon pa'i 'thil du//sen rtses ri rab bris la//de'i nang gi zur gyi tshal yi ge hri bzhi'i dbusu sngags kyi tshig bris te/gnod cing zlas la dbang du byed pa'i mchod yin no//oM badzra tro tA ma hA pa la ha na
35a4(rgyud): da ha/pa tsa pi taM sha yA/yo s+yo ma ra ya hU~M phaT/grogs mkhar ba'i rnam lnga dang sres pa las/'tho gang tsam gyi ba su ka byas te//ka ra bi ra'i lcug mas chu rtor zhing //gu gul gyis bar ma chad par 
35a5(rgyud): gdugs te/dkar gsum byi rtor ma byin pa ni/nyi ma bdun chu'i rgyun 'bebs//par byed do//ci ste 'bebs na nyi ma bdun la duM bur dum bur byas te//bum par bcug nas chu 'o la skur na char 'bebs
35a6(rgyud): bar 'gyur ro//char 'bab pa'i go tsha go'o//oM ga ga a ri ga ga swA hA//sngags 'dis gdong krus te rdob la lan bdun zlas pa byas na/su yang rung ba'i sgo'i bu gar btug na bcings pa byed par 'gyuro//
35a7(rgyud): oM mu kha ti s+than ti//mug gi tsh+tsha na ti/mu ka ka rtan ti lam ga lam ka nA/mu tra pu re she na/bde ba tan ta s+ya mu kha b+ha na d+ha mi swA hA//khyu mchog gi lci ba sa la ma lhung ba bla blangs te//gang su yang rung ba'i
35a8(rgyud): bu gar bzhugs par bya na//tha snyad brjod pa'i kha bcings par 'gyur te//klags pas grub po//oM na mo ni kha be rA bA ya//tsi ra tsi ri ke ru ke ru ta ru be na/mu shi ga nA tun ta/b+han d+ha mi mar k+Sha ta//
35b1(rgyud): bi ga la sar thA;ri kro bA rA hA//d+hi nam /ri ri si ri /khi li khi li swA hA//sngags 'dis lag pa ya cig gam rdol lan nyi shu rtsa gcig yongs su bzlas na//byim 'am zhing ngam/ldum ra'i 
35b2(rgyud): zur bzhir rdo ba bcug na nye bar 'tshe ba med par 'gyuro //yang na dug dang /ske tse dang /lan tsha dang //yungs dkar dang la pug gis bon dang /da du ra'i sa bon dang /nag po chen po'i sa bob gyi rdzas 'di dag

35b5(rgyud): gi lje ba la mngon par sngags te//chu'i ngogasu bcug na//zho rnams chud zos par gyur na//mo ya rtor bas grol bar 'gyur ro//oM re ban ta/si mi ni t+ye lo t+ya a ti pa ye swA hA//ku ru
35b6(rgyud): ku ru;gad ts+tsha gang ts+tsha/b+ha k+Sha ya b+hak+Sha ya/ne tra ra gis ri si ri/na sha ya na sha yA/hU~M hU~M phaT phaT hri hri swA hA/mig nad la byab pa'i sngagaso /oM a ru ne hU~M phaT swA hA//chu la yongs su bzlas te//mig
35b7(rgyud): krus te mig nad 'joms par 'gyur ro//smad pa ni rtsa bdum bu bdun du byas te//lag pa la bcings na/rims 'joms par 'gyuro//a la marga'i rtsa ba bcings na rims nyin bzhi pa 'joms par
35b8(rgyud): 'gyur ro//shing pa la sa ta pa rna blangs te sgo na rtsa 'am lam du sbas na tshang rnam par nyams par 'gyur te//ton na ni grol bar 'gyur ro//au na mo tsan d+ha a ku sha ku lu swA hA//phug ron gyi rgyun dang /
36a1(rgyud): @#//lham bu chu gsher dang stang zil dang /gu gul dang /shing tsa dang /chu srin dang sen ma dang ru rta dang /gse shing gi 'bras bu ste//'di rnams de bzhin du bu ram dang /sbyar la yongasu sbyar te//zong gi snod
36a2(rgyud): thams cad bdugs na/tshong pa rnaMs rnyed par bzang por 'gyuro //oM na mo b+ha ga ba te//pus ka tsa yun te tsi ro mi ri rib ta ke a mu kaM pa sham ya swAhA//ru rta shu tag dbang po'i gnas//yungs dkar ta ra ka ri
36a3(rgyud): me tog //sug smel de bzhin me tog /sa rdza ra rdza cha snyams byas//de dag gi ni rjes la bdugs /tshong ni myur du 'ong bar 'gyur /yi ge ya gnyis kha sbyar byas//dbus su ming ni sbyar byas te rlung gis khyer ba'i
36a4(rgyud): shing los bzung //de'i lo ma la dril bya/kha'i sgo'i snyugu dang /snam tsa dug dang ske tse dang //dur khrod sol ba mnyam ldan pas legs par gsangs te bris nas mchod//skyegs dang mig dmar
36a5(rgyud): nyon mongs pa//chub ma'i rlung la sbyar ba 'am/chu bo'i rgyun la sgyur byas na/dgra ni sod pa nyid du 'gyur //mtshan gcig pa'i ras chen po song ste//hU~M hU~M hU~M phaT phaT phaT //ces bas bdag nyid chen
36a6(rgyud): pos sngags bzlas par bya ste//skrad pa rab tu sbyor me'o //ba'i lji ba sa la ma lhung ba la //sor bcu drag pa'i gzugs byas la //dur khrod kyi ras la mi mngon par bris la//de'i nang du bcug la khro ba dang //
36a7(rgyud): bcas pas de'i kha bag zan gyis bka' la //ro'i sgra'i srad bus bkris te/yang khro bas lan bdun bzlas pa'i rtun shing gis de'i snun par byas na gnan par 'gyur ro//lag pa g.yon pa long ros
36a8(rgyud): gzugs rnyan bris te//mar me'i rtse mo la bdugs na dbang du byed pa'i cho ga'o//rlung gi phyogs su man+Tal nye bar byugs te//gro ga'i 'dab ma ltas sbyin gyi ming dang spel ba bris la//bag zan gyi gong bu'i nang du
36b1(rgyud): bcug ste/r+ya rog la byin na skrad par 'gyur ro//rtsa rnga mkhan gyis lag pa phyis pa'i sa la 'jim pa la snod byas te//stong rtsa brgyad yongs su bzlas te//dbugs mi 'byin par chu'i nang du bzhag na//
36b2(rgyud): grong brtan par 'gyur ro//de nas dgra bsad par 'dod na/sra tshil lam bag zan la gzugs ran byas la//chos gos kyi ras kyis gzugs ran de'i gdong nas rtsams te rkang pa'i bar du kris la
36b3(rgyud): legs par sbas la//de'i steng du lag pa gzhag la ming dang spel ba'i sngags stong du gcig gis chad pa bzlas nas//lha'i khang pa'i phyogs gcig tu gzhag ste//snying khar seng ldeng gi phur bu
36b4(rgyud): btab la//phur pa'i steng du dus gsum du chu tshun blugs shing //khro bas yongasu bzlas na rlag par 'gyuro//yungs dkar lnga khrag dang sres pa dang /'bru mar tsha ba dang pal ta ka sres pa dang /lcags
36b5(rgyud): kyi phye ma rnams sres shing sbyar te dgra'i ming nas bzung nas sbyin sreg stong rtsa gcig byas nas//de skad cig la sod par 'gyuro/yungs dkar ser rkyang khrag dang sres pas 'bru mar tsha ba dang
36b6(rgyud): pa la ta ka sres la//dga'i ming nas bzung nas/dur khrod kyi me la lan rgyad stong sbyin sreg byas na/sgrub bya rjes byed kyis bsod par 'gyur te//khrag skyugs par 'gyur zhing //rims sam bzer
36b7(rgyud): na dkyil 'chi bar 'gyur ro//dur khrod kyi thal ba la dgra'i gzugs byas te//rkang pa g.yon pas gnan la sgra dkrol zhing khros pas ltor ltas te//dgra'i ming nas zung nas/lan brgya stong
36b8(rgyud): sbyin sreg byas na/dgra'i myur du 'chi bar 'gyur ro//til mar po dang me tog ya ku dmar dang sres te lan brgya stong sbyin sreg byas na /mchog tu bskal pa bzang por 'gyur ro//til kar po dang 'bras dkar
37a1(rgyud): //mo mar dang sres pas /lan brgya stong sbyin sreg byas na//nor dang 'bru 'phel bar 'gyur ro//thams cad du bla ni ma yin no//ka ra bi ra'i me tog gnyis//de bzhin du ni tsan dan gnyis ru rta shing 
37a2(rgyud): tsha yung ba ser//me tog shing dang yung ba nag//de bzhin du na ud pa la gsum//pad ma ge zar de bzhin te//kri yang ku dang me tog brgya//de bzhin don grub dka' po dang /nir kun til dang 'byung po'i sgra/
37a3(rgyud): g+hi wang ge zer tsha ba gsum//de bzhin chu bo'i sa bon te/ldong ros dang ni 'bras bu gsum//nim pa'i lo ma u shi ri//ka ran dza'i 'bras bu dang //shing kun pa la ta ka ste//'di ltar nyi shu rtsa lnga ni/
37a4(rgyud): cha mnyam gsum du byas su//rgyal dang nyi ma la sbyar nas//ba'i rnam lnga la btags te//ji ltar ri su byas nas su//nyi ma la ni skam par bya//mar ngo'i tshes brgyad khyad par la dka ta ste/
37a5(rgyud): mchog tu myur bar gnas gyur//pas /rang 'dod lha'i mdun du ni//brgyad stong du ni bzlas par bya//cho ga 'di ltar sngar byas nas//rab gnas sbyin sreg rdzogs par bya//chang dang mig

37a8(rgyud): ba na//pral par byugs na rgyal bar 'gyur//skra'i rtse mo la byugs na bud med dbang du 'gyur bas//gzhonu ma rnams 'dzin cing bskal pa bzang por 'gyur ro//bud med bu mi byin pa rnams kyi lte ba
37b1(rgyud): la ngang skye gnas su byugs na bu 'byung rab 'gyuro//mdze nad thams cad la ba glang gi chu dang lhan cig 'thungs na bde legasu 'gyur ro//dang la mi chags pa rnams la dpyid kyi dus su ba'i rnam lnga dang lhan 
37b2(rgyud): cig tu 'thungs na /dmal chags shing bu dang ldan par 'gyuro //khrag gis gzer ba la 'bras kyi chu lhan cig 'thungs na zug zer rab tu zhi bar 'gyuro //mig na ba la mgo la byugs na mig nad zhi bar
37b3(rgyud): 'gyuro //'chi ba'i dug la 'tshil mas skus na 'chi dug med par 'gyuro//tag bcas nas seng ge dang /dom la sogs pa'i nags kyi bdag po rnams dang /sdig pa rta rnga mo la sogs pa dang /

38a2(rgyud): byed pa yin//thog ma tha ma rnam spangs pa//bdag cing shes dang /mtshungs zhing dag/ri dgas lte bar ba sngags te//mdzes pa'i bla rtsi yang dag blang//

38a5(rgyud): zla bas lus ni rgyas par byed //duru kas ni thams cad bsrung//ri dgas lte bas nad 'joms te//sman gyis zhi bar byed pa yin//rtag tu zhi 'am mnyam gyur pa'i//

38b1(rgyud): skyo bzhi dang ni rnam bral lo//rab mdzes dri ma med cing//cung zad shel dang mtshungs pa'i 'od//dag cing shel ltar 'gyur ba nyid//lus ni bcing ba'i mchog yin te//

39a1(rgyud): //sten par bya dngos grub dus lo gnyis kyang//mkhas pas rtag tu yang dag bsrang//lo bcu gnyis ni yang dag rdzogs//gnyer ma skra dkar rnam par spangs//sna tshogs nad las rnam par grol//

39b1(rgyud): te mkha' dbyings 'o ma'i rgya mtsho mdzes pa yi//bdud rtsi sgrubs pa tsam gyis ni//de la skyes pa'i lha mo tshang//nu mo 'dod pa'i gzugs can ma//nyi ma 'char ka'i mdog 'dra zhing//rgya skyegs khu ba lta bu'i 'od//
39b2(rgyud): rin chen kun gyis brgyan pa'i lus//pad ma'i mdog dang 'tshungs pa'i 'od//yi ge lo las byung ba'i sku//mdzes pa'i phyag ni bcwo brgyad ste//lha mo sna tshogs bcud 'dzin cing//'jig rten gsum po dbang sdud
39b3(rgyud): ma//g.yas pa mda' lugs kyu//thod pa rdo rje rgyal mtshan dang//de bzhin mu tig phreng dril bu//dgug pas mchog ni rab ster ba'o//g.yon pa'i phyag gis phub dang gzhu//zhags pa dang ni kha traM kha//ril ba spyi blug+sa
39b4(rgyud): mdung dang ni//the bo paM wa grang phreng bcas//gzhon zhi lang ma cho phun tshogaso//lha mo mdzes pa'i spyan gsum can//man d+ha ra ba'i dbus byung nas//chu bo kun du sgyur nas ni//'o ma'i

39b8(rgyud): ro gang don yod grub pa ste//gang zhig rlung gi shugs bdag nyid//chang med pa la ye shes cing//rnam shes kyang ni ci zhig 'gyur//rnam shes ye shes phun tshogs yas//chang gis
40a1(rgyud): @#//'gro kun rmongs par 'gyur//gnas dang zhing dang tshan do ho//'dus pa dang ni dur khrod du//mchod bya mchod byed bral ba yin//bdud rtsi mchod yon mchog yin te//rgyud dang rgyud gzhan

40a7(rgyud): dug 'gyur the tsom med//sngags kyi dngos grub mi skye bo//su zhig 'chang gis myos 'gyur ba//de la bgegs ni mang po 'byung//sngags pa chang gis myos 'gyur bas//'dod pa rkam zhing khrig par

40b1(rgyud): de la nad dang mya ngan 'jigs//'jigs par byed pas rnam rtses nas//sdig can dmyal bar 'khrid par 'gyur//bla ma la 'khu bla ma smod//sems can 'khu la sbyin ming zhing//bdud rtsi de nyid dug de

40b5(rgyud): de bzhin bur shing skyes//sa stengs gang dag skyes rnams te//mar ti rnam pa lnga bshad de//pa shwi rnam par brgyad du brjod//go 'di rnam pa bdun yin te//rim pa 'di dag shes par bya//
40b6(rgyud): rno dang kha dang tsha ba dang//dngar dang 'jam par skye ba ste//sna tshogs yul du rnam skyes nas//chang gi ming du rab tu grags//chu lha dga'o ba su ka/der ni bdan du sgom par bya//me tog dang ni gum gum bdug/
40b7(rgyud): rdor ma byin nas rtsam par bya//cho ga rdzogs par byas nas ni//smyo byed mchog ni skye bar 'gyur//gang tshe grub pa'i byang sems sam//nyin rer bya ba yin//sbyor ba 'di ni mchog tu mdzes//
40b8(rgyud): grub pa'i myos byed yid dga' ba//shi dri'i sman ni zho gnyis dang//skyu ru ra yang tsha bcu dang//de bzhin chu ni breng gang nyid//pho ba ris ni drug bcu gsum//bu ram sum nyis gcig bzung ste//
41a1(rgyud): //'di dag gcig bu sbyar byas te//'di ni grub pa'i chang zhes brjod//nyi ma'i 'od la btsa' bar bya//skur du nga'i nyos byed do//rta ta ka'i me tog bzung/tsu ta'i me tog so ba dang//ma la ya dang sa ri ba/
41a2(rgyud): rdo dreg dri ku pa la ka/'di dag cha ni mnyam pa ma//bzhi pa ru ni rab tu rtag/chu srang sum bcu rtsa gnyis dang/bu ra sbrang rtsi brgyad du 'gyur//nyi ma gsum na chang de nyid//'byung ba ru ni shes par bya//me tog da ta ka'i chad do/
41a3(rgyud): g+han rda pad pa pho ba ris//cod dang na ga ge zar bzung//dwa dmi 'bras bu de bzhin tshe//pi pi ling dang sgo las bcas/bu mar srang ni gcig nyid te/chu ni bdun 'gyur sbyin par bya//myos byed dri ni zhim pa dang//bsil
41a4(rgyud): ba'i bdag nyid du ni 'gyur//pa tra ka'i chang ngo//shing tsha e la na la taM/legs mthong tam la ru pa ni//sha kha ra dang lhan cig sbyar//mkhas gcig tu sres byas la//nyi ma'i 'od la lan bdun
41a5(rgyud): gtso/grub pa'i chang ni bla na med/kha ra chang ngo//sri gu'i rtsa ba las/byung chu//sbrang rtsi dang ni lhan cig ldan//mkhas pas rdzas ni dag bya'i phyir//cha rgyad du ni rab tu sbyin//sbrang rtsi lhag par sbyar la btso
41a6(rgyud): de yang 'bras bu gsum gur gum bla rtsi dang ni ga bur dang//g+ha da pa dra pas ka ru//dbye par rag tu sbyar byas te//thams cad brgya phyi nas ni//dbye ba'i don du sbyar bar bya//nyin zhag god bzhin khyad
41a7(rgyud): du//'bru'i dbus su bzhug par bya//chang ni smin par gyur pa dang//myangs na bla rtsi'i dril 'gyur//so bon dza ni shun pa dang//sbrang rtsi'i bzhi 'gyur grub par 'gyur//dbye ba gnyis pa ga pur dang//bla rtsi 'dza'
41a8(rgyud): ting lar bcas//ri dgas gregs par bcas pa ni//chang ni dri ma med par 'gyur//d+ha da ka'i me tog dang//b+ha li phye dang sbrang rtsir bcas//de la slar yang mtshan nyid 'dis//bzhi chas lhas pas grub par 'gyur
41b1(rgyud): g+han pa tra srang phyed ni//brgyad cha dang ni skyar bar bya//'di ltar 'di nyid bsgrubs pa 'dis//zla rer sbyar bar bya//sna tshogs chang ni dbye ba ni//yul dang rjes 'thun 'gyur zhes bya//'di ltar 'chang gi dbye ba ni//
41b2(rgyud): rgyud dang rgyud gzhan las shes bya//myos byed las gzhan mchod pa med//mar me sbyin sreg mchog ma yin//bla ma dang bral chos med te//myos byed las gzhan dam tshig ni//
41b3(rgyud): gar yang 'byung bar 'gyur ba min//bdag nyid bsod nams tshogs kyis sam//la las bla ma mnyes pas rnyes//'di ni myos byed nges par bstan pa'i rim pa phye ba ste nyi shu drug pa'o////
41b4(rgyud): ////de nas gzhan yang sngags btu ba'i chog mchog ni rab tu bshad//sa'i cha ni 'di nyid 'ong bar//dri'i chus ni nye bar byug//dben pa'i gnasu legs spas te//me tog spos
41b5(rgyud): kyis nyams dga' byas//sngags pas tsa ru bza' ba dpyad//de bzhin bdud rtsi'i btung ba dpyad//rang gis lhag pa'i ltar sbyar bas sngon du rtor ma sbyin byas te//mkha' 'gro ma kun mnyam
41b6(rgyud): sbyor ba'i//slob dpon legs par mnyam gzhag pas gru gsum dkyil 'khor nyams dga' ba//ri mo gsum gyis phyi rol skor//chos 'byung skyed mos tshal chen po//sno tshogs me tog gis ni mdzes/der
41b7(rgyud): ni a li ka li dbye//sde tshan brgyad po rims bzhin bri//'dod pa drag po de bzhin nyid//thub pa mda' dang bzugs me nyid//re'u bzhi bcu rtsa dgur ni//man ngag bzhin du dgod par bya//a rtsogs yi ge nas
41b8(rgyud): rtsams te//yi ge ha'i bar du sngags//bskor ba'i rnam par sbyar ba ni//gsang ba'i bdag pos shes par gyis//re'u mig dang po'i lnga ba bzung //de ni re'u mig bcu pa nyid//re'u mig dgu pa'i nad
42a1(rgyud): @#//gsum pa yis//steng du thig las rnam par rgyan//re'u mig bdun pa'i lnga pa bzung //me'i sa bon 'og tu brgyan //dang po nas ni bzhi pa yis //brgyan pa 'dod pa mo'i
42a2(rgyud): mchog//bdun pa'i gnyis pa'i yige bzung //dang po re'u mig bcu gcig blang /re mig dgu pa'i lnga pa bcung //yi ge dbu yis/'og tu brgyan//re'u bdun pa'i bzhi pa bzung //
42a3(rgyud): re mig dang po bcu gcig pas//mgo bo la ni brgyan par bya/re mig dgu pa'i lnga pa gzung //dang po bas ni 'og tu rgyan//re mig lnga pa'i lnga pa bzung //re mig bdun bzhi pa 
42a4(rgyud): blang //ri mig cu gcig pas rgyan cing //lnga ba yis ni 'og tu rgyan//lag gnyisu ni bzlas par bya//re mig lnga pa'i gnyis pa bzung //re'u mig gi bcu gcig 
42a5(rgyud): pa //bzhi pa dang ni yang dag ldan//snying po sngags ni rtag bzlas pas //dngos grub yon tan kun gyi gnas/re'u mig gi bdun pa yi //yige bzung bar bya //re'u mig 
42a6(rgyud): dag ni dgu pa'o//lta ba yis ni 'og tu rgyan //dang po bzhi pas rab tu brgyan//bdun pa dang ni bdun pa yis //lo gsu thig le gnyis sbyin bya//re mig dag ni bdun pa yis//
42a7(rgyud): yige bzhi pa bzung byas te//lan gnyis su ni bzlas par bya//slar yang yige de nyid bzung //dbyangs kyi yi ge lnga pas 'og tu rgyan//re'u mig dgu pa'i gsum pa'o nas//thig
42a8(rgyud): le yis ni rnam par brgyan//lan gnyis su ni bzla par bya//re mig dag ni btun pa'i lnga pa yis//yige gnyis pa bzung bar bya//re'u mig cu gcig pa dag gi bzhi pa nyid ni/
42b1(rgyud): bzung bar bya//sngon du phyag btsal sbyin bar bya//rtag par rgyun du bzlas nas ni //bskal pa zang dngos grub byed pa'o//re'u mig dag ni bdun pa yi//yi ge gnyis pa yang dag bzung /
42b2(rgyud): bcu gcig dang po'i yi ge ni//me'i bus ni 'og tu rgyan //bdun pa gnyis pa'i yi ge bzung //dpyangs ni bcu gcig pa'i brgyan//re'u mig dag ni dgu pa yis //yi ge lnga ba yang dag bzung /
42b3(rgyud): dbyangs yig cu gnyis pa yis rgyan//bdun pa'i dang po yig ni//yang mchog gi'i go 'phang bzung //re'u mig ni lnga pa yi//yi ge bzhi pa yang dag bzung //dbyangs kyi bzhi pas rgyan 
42b4(rgyud): pa'o//re'u mig dag ni dgu ba yis//yi ge bzhi pa bzung bar bya//dbyangs yig cu gcig pa yis rgyan//bdun pas bzhi pa'i yi ge bzung //dbyangs yig lnga pas 'dzes par byas
42b5(rgyud): dgu la ni gsum pa yis /de bzhin mgo la mdzes par bya//de ni lan gnyis brjod par bya//yi ge mchog ni grol bar byaster//lnga pa'i yi ge gnyis pa bzung //re'u
42b6(rgyud): mig bcu gcig pa yis ni/bzhi pa nyid ni sbyar bar bya//re'u mig dag ni bdun pa yis//yi ge gsum pa bzung bar bya//re'u mig dag na bdun pa yis //yi ge bdun pas 'og
42b7(rgyud): tu rgyan dbyangs yig gnyis pas brgyan par bya//re'u mig dag ni 'dun pa yi //yi ge bzhi pa bsdu bar bya//dbyangs yig gnyis pas brgyad par bya//thor mar yang ni gsal byed
42b8(rgyud): sbyin //mkha' 'gro ma'i snying dga' ba //rtag tu sgom zhing bzlas byas na/dngos grub kun gyi mchog rab ster//thog mar yi ge o sbyin bya //bdun pa'i gsum pa yang bzung //dbyangs
43a1(rgyud): @#//kyi lnga pas yang dag rgyan//re'u mig dag ni dgu pa yi//yi ge gnyis pa bzung byas te//de nyid la ni gsum pa yis//mgo la rgyan du byas pa yin//re'u mig dag ni lnga pa yi//yi ge
43a2(rgyud): bzhi pa yang dag blang//dbyangs kyi gsuM pas rgyan pa'o//re'u mig dag ni bdun pa'o//yi ge gsum pa bzung bar bya//ri'u mig dang ni dgu pa'o//yi ge gnyis pa bzung bar te//de nyid la ni
43a3(rgyud): gsum pas brgyan//re'u mig dag ni bdunapa'o//yi ge bzhi pa bzung bar bya//dbyangs yig lnga pas 'og tu rgyan//dgu pa'i gsum pa'i yi ge yis//thig le ru ni brgyan pa yin//de ni 
43a4(rgyud): rims bzhin gnyisu bzlas//re'u mig dag ni lnga yi//yi ge gnyis pa yang dag blang//re'u mig dag ni bcu gcig pa'i//bzhi pa'i yi ge mchog sbyar dbang //re'u mig gcig gi yi ge bzung /
43a5(rgyud): dbyangs kyi bdun pa 'og tu sbyin//re'u mig dag ni bdud pa yi//yi ge mnyam pa yang dag blang//re'u cig dag gi ni lnga pa yi//yi ge bzhi pa yang dag mdzes//sngags ni lan
43a6(rgyud): gnyis brjod//par bya/re'u mig dag ni bdun pa yi//yi ge bzhi pa bzung par bya//dbyangs ni lnga pas 'og tu sbyar//zla ba phyed pas thig les rgyan//de'i tshig ni lan gnyis brjod//re'u mig
43a7(rgyud): dag ni lnga pa yis//yi ge gnyis pa bzung bar bya//re'u mig dag ni bcu bcig pa'o//zhi ba sbyar bas bde mchog ster//gsal byed dang por yang dag gzhag/re'u mig gcig gis yi ge bzung /
43a8(rgyud): dbyangs kyis bdun pas sbyar bar bya//re'u mig dgag ni bdun pa yi yig bzhi pa bzung bar bya//re'u yig dag ni lnga pa yi//bzhi pa yi ni sbyar bar bya//dbyangs yig gnyis par yang dag brgyan/re'u
43b1(rgyud): mig lnga pa'i dang po bzung//re'u mig drug pa'i dang po blang//tshig ni lan gnyis brjod par bya//re'u mig dag ni bdun pa yi//yi ge bzhi pa bzung bar bya//dbyangs kyi lnga pa 'og tu brgyan//zla ba
43b2(rgyud): phyed pa'i theg les brgyan//thams cad lan gnyis brjod par bya//re'u mig dag ni lnga pa yi//yi ge gnyis pa bzung bar bya//re'u mig dag ni cu gcig pa'o//bzhi pa sbyar bas dbang phyug mchog/
43b3(rgyud): thog mar gsal byed yang dag sbyar//yi ge mchog ni bsgrub par bya//re'u mig dag na ni sum bcu pa nyid kyis//dbyangs kyi yi ge bzung bar bya//dbyangs yig gnyis pas sbyar bar 'gyur//re'u mig
43b4(rgyud): dag ni lnga pa yis yi ge bzhi pa bzung nas ni//gzhag pas go 'phang mchog tu 'gyur//re'u mig dag ni dgu pa yi//yi ge bzhi bzung mchog dbang phyug//re'u mig dag ni bdunapa yi/
43b5(rgyud): yi ge bzhi pa bzung bar ba//dbyangs kyi sum bcu pa yis ni//mog bo la ni brgyan par bya//mkhas pas bdun pa bdun pas ni//logasu thig le gnyisu gzhag//re'u
43b6(rgyud): mig ni bdun pa'o//yi ge gnyis pa bzung par bya//dbyangs kyi gsum spyi bos rgyan//re'u mig dag ni dgu ba yi//yig brgyad pa blang bar bya//de nyid kyi ni mgo bzhi pa sbyin byangs kyi gnyis
43b7(rgyud): pa sbyar bar bya//yi ge mchog ni yin par brjod//re'u mig dag ni dgupa yi//yi ge lnga pa bzung bar bya//dbyangs ni gnyis pa mdzes par bya//re'u mig bdun pa dang po ni//gzhag pas 
43b8(rgyud): dngos grub thams cad ster//re'u mig dag ni bdud pa'i yi ge bzhi pa blang bar bya//dbyangs ni lnga pa sbyar byas te//zla ba phyed pa thig les brgyan//lan gnyis brjod pas mchog dbang phyug//
44a1(rgyud): @#//lnga pa'i yi ge gnyis pa bzung //re'u mig cu gcig pa nyid kyis //bzhi pa yis ni sbyar byas te//yang dag go 'phang mchog yin no//bla ma'i man ngag lam gyis ni//sngags btu
44a2(rgyud): ba ni shes par bya//yang dag bstan dang rnam bral ba'i //glegs bam bklags tsam gyis sngags las bskal pa bye ba brgya ru yang //dngos grub go 'phang 'byung mi 'gyur//tshes bcu'i 
44a3(rgyud): ni dug dag la //sna tshogs me tog la sogs dang //bza' ba btung ba mnyam ldan pas //rdo rje rnal 'byor ma mchog bya//sngags nyid rnal 'byor ma'i gzugs //ci ste go 'phang mchog
44a4(rgyud): 'dod na //sreg ni yongs su gtong yang bla //'chi bar nyam ngar 'gro yang bla'i //rtag tu dam tshig bsrung bar bya//'jig rten gsum po rgyu ma'i rgyu'i//'gro kun g.yon las byung
44a5(rgyud): bas na//rnal 'byor ma rtag g.yon la spyod //rkang pa g.yon la 'dor //lag pa g.yon gyis mchod par bya//g.yon pa yis ni tshim dang bza'//rigs la yang ni/
44a6(rgyud): kun spyod pa //rigs gcig tu ni rnam par rtag //kun rtags kun las rnam grol zhing //gnyis pa thams cad rnam par spang //bla mas sangs rgyas bla ma chos //bla ma 
44a7(rgyud): dge 'dun de bzhin te//sangs rgyas chos dang mnyam sbyor ba'i //mkha' 'gro ma'i dra ba'i sngon //de bzhin sangs rgyas go 'phang ni//'dod pas bla ma mnyes par bya//'di ni sngags
44a8(rgyud): btu pa'i cho ga rim pa phye ba ste nyi shu bdun pa'o////de nas de phyir sreg gi las kyi khyad par rab bshad bya//bsgrub pos rgyal srid phyed du ni //sngags ni stong phrag bcu
44b1(rgyud): ru bzlas//sngar bshad pa'i cho ga yis sbyin sreg las ni rtsam par bya//bsgrub bya'i ni ming spel ba'i//sha chen 'o mar sbyar ba yis//rnam rtog med par sbyin sreg bya//de dag dbye bar mi bya'o//
44b2(rgyud): ci ste dngos grub mchog 'dod nas/yongs rdzogs rgyal po nyid du 'gyur//ba glang mi dang lce spyang sha//chang dang 'o ma mnyam sres pas//sreg lugs mchog ni sbyin byaste//rtag tu mub gcig sbyin
44b3(rgyud): sreg byas//grong khyer mchog ni thob 'gyur te//dpa' ldan rgyal po chen por 'gyur//tsher ma'i me ni rab sbyar la//dug khrag 'bru mar tsha ba dang //de bzhin skra dang phu ba mar ldan//mi yi
44b4(rgyud): rus pas sbyin sreg byas//sgra grol khros pa'i dbang gyur pas//lho phyogs kha ltas gcer bu ba//sngags pa//gos ni nag po gon//drag po'i las la nyin phyed dam//
44b5(rgyud): mtshan phyed na ste gdol pa med//bsgrub bya ming dang spel ba'i sngags//nga ro dgra po rjod bya zhing //rtse gcig sems kyis sbyin sreg byas//dpung byas stobs kyang rlag 'gyur na//gzhan
44b6(rgyud): rnams la ni smos ci dgos//de nas stobs kyi dregs pa yis//dgra bo bsad par shes par bya//rlung gi phyogs su kha ltas te//sha za'i me ni rab sbar nas//khro'i gsheg pa
44b7(rgyud): gzhag//min pa la//nir ya sa'i 'bru mar sres//las ni mtshan no bdun sbyar ngan//the tsom med par song par 'gyur//dbye ba'i las zhes grags pa ni//smyo byed shing la me spar
44b8(rgyud): la//gsang sngags rig pa nim pa ni//lo ma sbrul gyi shun par bcas//khra dang 'ug pa'i chang sres te//brgya rtsa brgyad ni sbyin sreg byas//'jig rten thas cad dbye 'gyur te//snying 
45a1(rgyud): //sdug skye bo'i mnyen gyi spong //de nas dgug pa bshad bya ste//li khri mtshungs ma'i 'od sgoms te//sgrub bya gcer bu rlung phyogs nas//steng ltas g.yo bar dmigs nasu//stan 
45a2(rgyud): las rol pa'i gnas las gnas//gsang sngags yi ge dza bzlas te/zhabs pa lcags kyus rab 'bad pas //bsgrub pa'i snying kha'i pad mar bdug //skye gnas pad ma dbugs byas
45a3(rgyud): nas//nges par khams gsum du dbang du 'gyur //thod pa dum bu gcig pa 'am /ma chag stug cing mdzes pa la//bsgrub bya'i gzugs ni rnam par bri//de nas bsgrub bya'i ming brjod do//
45a4(rgyud): da ru ra'i shing nyid dang //ka ra bi ra khrag tu ni //yang dag sbyar ba'i sbyin sreg byas//gro ga la ni bsgrub bya gzugs rang khrag g+hi wang sres pas bri//bsgrub bya'i gos kyis g.yogs nas
45a5(rgyud): ni/gsang sngags shing sbyin sreg bya//sngags kyi nga ror bcas lugs kyis//me tog re re rab bzung nas//nyi ma bdun du sbyin sreg bya//gang gis yid la 'dod pa bzung //
45a6(rgyud): de nas gzhan yang bshad par bya/tsan dan dmar po'i shing las ni/gzugs byas nas rang khrag dang /g+hi wang gis ni ming bris te//de bzhin bsgrub bya'i snying khar gzhug//
45a7(rgyud): tsha ba gsum gyis lus la dbyug //zangs ma'i khab kyis dbyug byas la//snying kha lte ba'i gsang ba ste//bsgrub bya'i gnas gsum du dbug //gang zhig yid la 'dod pa de//mtshan phyed na ni 'gugs par 'gyur//
45a8(rgyud): gser mdog can gyi tsag gis skye/gnas kyi mtshan ma mngon bris la //de'i steng du lag pa g.yon pa bkab nas/brgya rtsa brgyad ni bzlas byas te//gang gi ming nas brjod nas sngags bzlas nas //skad
45b1(rgyud): cig nyid la 'od bar 'gyur//'chub mas khyer ba'i shing lo la//khra'i sgro rtsa dang 'chi mas bsgrub bya'i ming bris te//gyen du langs 'chub ma la bskur na//de'i mod la sod par 'gyur//spre'u'i rus pa
45b2(rgyud): pa la sor drug pa'i phur byas te//sngags bdun mngon par bzlas nas//gang gi sgor sbas pas//de'i rigs rgyud 'chad par 'gyur//ba ling rta dang bong bu dang //rnga mo ma he li gnas rnams su sbab
45b3(rgyud): na//de dag rlag par 'gyuro;;yi dgas kyi gos ma lhung ba blangs la//mar khu chen po dang lhan cig zhe de'i snod du sbyar te//mar ngo'i bcu bzhi'i khyad par la snying pa'i sngags bzlas shing mig smin dbab pa
45b4(rgyud): byas la//mig la byugs na;des mkha' 'gro thams cad mthong bar 'gyuro;;oM b+hu d+ha li ge swAhA//me dpal byab pa'i sngags so //glang po che'i lji ba blangs te//rus pal gyi
45b5(rgyud): khog pa'i nang du gzhug ste//khyim du 'dug par byin na//'dis ni 'dre gzhig pa la sogs yang rab tu zhi bar 'gyur ro//oM u da ke ma sha k+ta dz+wa ta//u da ka sa b+ha bA //te sha tu na ta sh+tsa ba
45b6(rgyud): k+Sha sh+tsa;in ro ban da ti mahA pa la //ma in ba sha ban Ta in dra pa sha ga ta gad tshan te//sur+ya o da ya swAhA//bzhi mdo'i rdo+e'i dum bu blangs la //'di ni lan ni nyi shu bzlas //phyogs bzhi
45b7(rgyud): ni bzhug par bya//sbrang bu 'chu rings rlog byas pas//yid can rnams ni bde bar 'gyur//bde bas chos ni thob 'gyur te//chos kyis kyang ni blang med 'gyur//'di ni sbyin sreg gi cho ga rim
45b8(rgyud): par phye ba ste nyi shu brgyad pa'o////de nas de nyid ci bzhin don //sgom pa yang dag dngos med rang bzhin te//dngos med spros dang bral ba yin//rang rig bdag nyid can gyi lus //
47a1(rgyud): @#//sgom pa 'ang sgom du med pa'o//gcig du ma'i rnam bral 'di//skad cig de nyid rab tu skye//ye shes 'di ni rang rig pa ngag gi spyod yul las 'das shing //kun mkhyen ye shes de'i dngos
47a2(rgyud): rmad du byung ba'i rnam par skyes//srid pa thams cad dngos med mnyam//gang zhig rting dpag dka' ba ste//rgyu dang rnam par bral ba yi/lhan cig skyes pa'i snang snyan ts+tsha rang zhin gyis ni 'od gsal ba//
47a3(rgyud): rnam par spyod pa'i spyod yul 'das//'o na ma the med cing zhi nges par skulan du med pa ste//brjod du med cing bgrang du med//stong pa nyid kyi rnam pa ni//dngos po kun gyi rang bzhin gnas//sgra sogs yon tan
47a4(rgyud): kun snang bral//'di ni sangs rgyas bde 'byung ba'o//byang chub gnas ni bde med pa//gang zhig 'gro la rna gnas pa//la lar sa ni ma bshad de//sor mnyam pa'i de nyid ni//rgyal ba yis
47a5(rgyud): ni gsungs pa yin//gnyis pa'i rnam pa las 'das gsal//bar du rig cing bskal pa bzang //dga' tshogs namakha'i dkyil 'khor khyab//ro ni yang dag rdzogs pa'i stobs//
47a6(rgyud): thams cad sems ni ngan song ni//sna tshogs rnam pa'i gzugs 'phro ba//'jig rten gsum na namakha' 'thib ltar yid la snang bar 'gyur//rnam kun gcig pa de mchog gi//rang
47a7(rgyud): bzhin lhan cig skyes mchod pa//rnam kun mtshan nyid kyis//de nyid dkyil 'khor shes par bya//rtsa kun mkha' 'gro ma rnams te//skye mched yan lag dkyil 'khor yin//thaMs cad ro
47a8(rgyud): gcig thob ba yis//he ru ka dpal rang bzhin rgyas//snang byed med cing thogs med pa//gnas med nus pa med pa ste//dngos po kun gyi rang bzhin phyir//dngos po kun la rtag tu gnas/rnam pa
47b1(rgyud): nyid du shes bya te;gang la sgoM pa rnal ma nyid //ngo bo nyid kyis dga' byas pas //'di ni rang 'byung 'char ba'o//de bas bsam mi khyab bsams pa//sna tshogs gcig tu shes par bya/gang la
47b2(rgyud): dngos dang dngos med kyi//bye brag rnam spangs bdag nyid kyi//dga' ba'i bdag nyid rtan gsal bas //namakha'i mtha' las khyab par ni//bdag nyid chen pos rtogs par bya//rnam pa sna
47b3(rgyud): tshogs dri med pa'i //dkyil 'khor dag ni 'khro bar snang //lhan cig skyes dga' bzhi pa'i ming//bde ba kun gyi gnas de yin//yang dag de nyid rig pa la //'dir ni thabs med
47b4(rgyud): shes rab med //bsgrubs pos sa gsum dkyil 'khor ni //rnam rtog nyid du shes par bya//chos dang long spyod rdzogs sprul pa//bde ba chen po zhes bya'i rim //bde ba chen po'i
47b5(rgyud): rang bzhin gyis //'khor lo bzhi po mnyam pa nyid//rtag tu dngos pos thobs cad la//bsgrub pos chags pa med par spyad//'khor ba'i 'dam gyi dbus su ni //gos myed ngan 'das
47b6(rgyud): pa'o//mdzes pa'i yon tan legs thob pas//ye shes ldan zhing brtson 'grus ldan//de nas skye bo bla ma gus//sangs rgyas dang ni mnyam lta zhing//rgyal ba'i chos ni shin du
47b7(rgyud): rtogs//bstan la legs par rab dad pas//deng 'dir snod du gyur pa ste//de la dka' drin sbyin par bya;de nas gzugs sogs dbyer med cing //khams gsum gcig tu
47b8(rgyud): shes pa na//rgyun mi 'chad par bsgrub po des //dga' bas kyang ni mchod rten bya//ci srid byang chub mchog ldan pas//de srid dri ma med nyid 'gyur //sgrub po thabs dang shes rab
48a1(rgyud): @#//dag|bla med 'bras bu thob par 'gyur//'di ni de kho na nyid bstan pa'i rim pa phye ba ste nyi shu dgu pa'o////de nas yang dag bshad bya ba|ri mo lha'i gzugs rnams kyi //sku'i tshad kyi 
48a2(rgyud): mtshan nyid ni//gsang ba yi ni bdag po nyon//tshangs pa'i ri mo sogs gzugs kyi//cha lnga yin gdan yin no//dum bu gsuM can sku lus kyi//cha mnyam pas ni zhal byas te//zhal ni cu gnyis

53b2(rgyud): mdzad//de ni snying rje chen po'i dbang sgyur 'phags pa rnams kyi rtag tu myur bar bkra shis yin//'di ni rtor ma'i mchod pa bstan pa'i rim par phye ba ste so gnyis pa'o////de nas ye shes
53b3(rgyud): 'byung ba yi//dngos grub sngo pa bshad par bya//sna tshogs tshul gyi thabs rnams ni//dngos grub rnams kyi rgya yi mchog//phyi dang nang du bcas pa'i lus//namakha' lta bu dri ma med//'di ltar bdag nyid
53b4(rgyud): mkha' mtshungs par//rtag tu 'gro ba'i bdag nyid mthong//lus med thog ma tha ma med//sgra la sogs pa'i yon tan bral/gnyis pa las ni rnam grol ba/rnaM pa kun tu cir yang gnas//dngos
53b5(rgyud): med dngos po la rten nas//rten pa med pa'i dngos por bya//yid med yid ni byas nas su//cung zad tsam yang mi bsamo/stan la gnas nas bla ma yi//rgyud pas brtan par byas nas ni/
53b6(rgyud): de bzhin namakha' dang yang mtshungs pa'i/ro gcig sems ni sgoM par bya//bsam gtan gzugs las rnam par grol//rtog ge sbyor ba rnam par spangs/sems byung sems su brtan gyur pa//'gro la
53b7(rgyud): 'ang rang bzhin tshul de bzhin/mkha' mtshungs namakhar yang dag gnas//shel nor bu dag pa bzhin//thog ma tha ma med pa'i gzugs spros bral dbang po'i spyod yul yin//'gyur ba med cing snang ba
53b8(rgyud): med/thams cad stong pa nyon mongs med/'gro ba'i sgron ma srid pa yi//mchid pa 'joms shing tshig brjod bral//yid kyi spyod yul ma yin te//gnyis las rna grol nyon mongs med/don dam grol bar rab
54a1(rgyud): @#//brtsol ba'i/'pho med de nyid phyag 'tshal//ci ltar de nyid la reg na//bsam pa kun gyis bsam du med//gang tshe bsam du med sems pa//de tshe bsam gyis mi khyab 'gyur//sems
54a2(rgyud): can ci bzhin bsam med de/bsam med de bzhin rgyal ba 'ang de//gang phyir bsaM bral sangs rgyas kyis//bsam pa 'di ni rab tu bshad//bsam gyis mi khyab sems de la//bsam pa kun gyi
54a3(rgyud): bgrod las 'das//sna tshogs rtogs pas rtog pa med//chags med bde ba chen po ste//rnam pa thaMs cad kyi ni mchog//dbang po kun nas rnam pa med//dngos dang dngos med rnam par spangs//
54a4(rgyud): bems po'i nyid phyir bdag nyid rig/ye shes rnam par mi mthong ngo/gzugs med gar ming gnas pa/'gyur ba med cing brtan pa'o//dngos po med pa'i chos rnams la//glang dang dor ba ga la zhig//
54a5(rgyud): blang bya dang ni dor bya'i//chos kun rmi lam ji bzhin no//dga' ba yongasu shes pa yis//shes rab pha rol phyin pa'i mchog//dga' ba'i 'bras bu la gnas pa'i//'di ni dngos po
54a6(rgyud): med byang chub po//gnyis po ro gcig dbyer med pa//rdzogs pa'i byang chub bsgrub byed pa//de nyid la ni bla na med pa'i//sangs rgyas kun rnams rab tu bzhugs//dbyer med rnam par yang dag rig//
54a7(rgyud): dga' ba'i bde ba chen po nyid/shes rab snying rje'i dbye ba ni/sgron ma dang ni 'od zer nyid/shes rab snying rje'i bye ba ni/sgron ma dang ni 'do zer bzhin//'di na gnyis mi phyed bdag nyid ni//ro gcig dngos
54a8(rgyud): po'i sems yin no//shes rab thabs ni mnyam sbyor bas//gang zhig gnas pa'i rdo rje'i sems/ci srid rdzogs pa'i byang chub dgur//bde bas sdom par byas nas ni//bsgrub po bde mchog rdzogs
54b1(rgyud): bar du//de srid de ni myong bar 'gyur//mdzes pa'i sgyu ma rnam 'phrul dag/la de shes cing bzhin dga'//skyo ba thas cad mnyam pa nyid du rab tu spyad //'jigs par byed pa 'ang med cing bde ba 'byung ba 'ang yod
54b2(rgyud): ma yin//de nyid kyis ni bsgrub po de bzhin bdag rjes 'gro//e ma ho/bde chen 'dod pa yi//sa gsum po ni gang ba ste//e ma ho'i zhi ba'i bde ba 'bab //sna tshogs mngon par byang chub rgyas //e ma ho/
54b3(rgyud): bde ba'i chen po|e ma ho ci lti ci ltar bza'//e ma ho lhan skyes bdag nyid che//chos rnams kun gyi ngo bo nyid/'gro ba chu zla bzhin du mthong ba dang //thos pa brag cha dang ni mtshungs par yang dag 'jug//
54b4(rgyud): sems ni mya ngan smyig sgyu lta bur lta bya zhing //'di ltar bza' dang /btung ba nam mkha' lta bu ste//nam mkha'i me tog dri dang mtshungs par snam pa dang //yid ni nyi zla bzhin du khyab par bya;
54b5(rgyud): yang dag gnas pa de 'ang ri rab dang ni mtshungs pa yin//gzhon nu ma'i rma lam de bzhin dmigs par bya//tha snyad dag ni sgyu ma mig 'phrul lta//bur bgrod //'di dag 'jig rten
54b6(rgyud): lhan skyes bde byung de bzhin te//srid pa'i rang bzhin bral zhing bsam gyis mi khyab gzugs//rtag tu 'tshar ba bde gshegs la mchog phyag 'tshal stod//mchod pa thams cad yongs spangs ste//
54b7(rgyud): bla ma'i mchod pa yang dag rtsam//de mnyes pas ni kun mkhyen pa'i //ye shes mchog ni thob par 'gyur//bla med mdzad pa'i slob dpon ni//rdo rje sems dpa' rab mchog na/de yis bsod
54b8(rgyud): nams ma byas kyi//dka' thub ma sten ci zhig yod//'jigs dang sdig pa rab spangs te//bden pa dang ni ldan pa nyid//dam tshig spyod pa bsrung byed pa;de la rim par rab brtan bya;he ru ka'i dpal mngon
55a1(rgyud): //spyod rgyud;klag gam 'chang ngam bris pas ni//dngos grub rdzu 'phrul bskal bzang dang /byang chub sems dpa' thob par 'gyur//mkha' 'gro ma'i dra ba sdom;dpal sdom 'byung ba'i rgyud la
55a2(rgyud): ni //gang tshe sgom zhing sems byed na//long spyod che zhing bde ba che//dpul ba'i sdug bsngang 'joms par 'gyur//mos pa du ma'i sems can la //spyod pa sna tshogs ston mdzad te//sna tshogs tshul gyis
55a3(rgyud): 'dul ba la//thabs kyang yang dag bstan pa yin//zab mo chos ni bstan pa la //gang zhig mos par ma gyur kyang //smad pa dag tu mi bya ste//stong nyid snying rje'i mi phyed pa'i //chos nyid thams cad
55a4(rgyud): bsam mi khyab//mkha' 'gro ma'i tshogs tshogs bsten pa'i //he ru ka dpal yang dag sbyor //sems can mos pas 'jug pa rnaMs //de dag kun la dga' ba ste//mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam
55a5(rgyud): sbyor ba'i//dpal he ru ka dpal go 'phang gnas 'di ni rim par phye ba sum bcu rtsa gsum po'////dpal he ru ka mngon par brjod pa'i rgyud las 'bum phrag gsum pa las /lhan
55a6(rgyud): cig skyes par 'byung ba'i rtog pa las btus pa/rnal 'byor ma thams cad kyi gsang ba klags pas 'grub pa //dpal sdom pa 'byung ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po rdzogs s+h+yo////
55a7(rgyud): shes rab dman zhing sbyangs pa chung mod kyis //rgyud kyi gzhung la dad pa brtan skyes pas //rang 'dod tsam du sgyur ba gang lags pa//'phags pa dang mkhas pa rnams kyis bzod par 'dzod//
55a8(rgyud): rgya kar gyi mkhan po gzhan la phan pa mtha' yas dang //bod kyi lo tsa ba rgyus ban de smon lam grags kyis sgyur cing zhug te//brtan la phab pa'o////rgya gar skad du/shri badzra d+ha ki
55b1(rgyud): ni ma mahA ta ma tra ra tsa//bdo skad du /rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro zhes bya ba bcom ldan 'das thugs rje chen po 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug la phya 'tshal lo//
55b2(rgyud): mkha' 'gro kun dngos sems dpa' ni//rdo rje mkha' 'gro bde ba mchog //gsang ba mchog gis dgos pa na//thams cad bdag nyid rtag tu zhugs//'di ni rang 'byung bcom
55b3(rgyud): ldan 'das //lhag pa yi ni lhan cig nyid//'byung gnas sgra tshul nges 'byung rnams //dam tshig spyod pa'i spyod yul yin//'jig rten gsuM du rnyed dka' ba//thog ma dbus mtha'
55b4(rgyud): yang dag gnas//'dod chags ma yin chags min bral//dbu mar yang mi dmigs so//bud med kun gyi sgyu ma'i rgya//'di ni gnyis med ye shes mchog //rnyed dang bde ba
55b5(rgyud): kun ma so pas//ngan par spyod pa 'ang 'grub par 'gyur//naM mkha' kun du rgyu 'grub pas //mkha' 'gro ma zhes kun du grags sna tshogs kun du phyag rgya ni//sna tshogs
55b6(rgyud): kun gyi phyag rgya yis//mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba'i//rdo rje mkha' 'gro zhes su grub//de nyid med sems rnal 'byor pa//lha la dmigs par byed pa dag//kun tu gzugs snyan
55b7(rgyud): nyid kyis ni//lug sogs rnal 'byor skye ba men//byang chub sems 'byin rnal 'byor gang//rnal 'byor dbang phyug rang nyid rtso//byang chub sems 'di rdo rje ste//sangs rgyas kun gyi
55b8(rgyud): rang bzhin no//'gro ba'i rtsa lag rang bzhin gyis//sna tshogs g.yogs te rnam bzhugs pas//de phyir 'bad pa thams cad kyis//sna tshogs tshul gyis mchod par bya//bdag
56a1(rgyud): //nyid sangs rgyas thes cad dang //rang gi lha'i sbyor ba yis//bdag nyid rab tu bsgrub par bya/'di yis sangs rgyas thams cad dang //dpa'o thams cad nyid dang ni//rdo rje 'dzin
56a2(rgyud): sapa thams cad du//tshe 'di nyid du 'grub 'gyur zhes//mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba//rdo rje mkha' 'gro bde ba'i mchog//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//dpal ldan bcom
56a3(rgyud): ldan 'das kyis gsungs//thams cad nam mkha'i ma nyid de//nam mkha' la ni mtshan nyid med//khams gsum pa ni ma lus pa//'gro ba kun sgyu ma lta ba yin//brtan pa dang ni g.yo ba yi//
56a4(rgyud): 'gro ba dmigasu med pa'o//mthong ba dang ni reg pa'i dngos //thaMs cad sgyu ma ji bzhin no//gang gis blun po 'ching 'gyur ba//'di mkhas de rnams rnam par rol//phyag
56a5(rgyud): rgya'i sbyor ba 'di yis ni//sa gsum lus bzla bar bya //gzhan du gal te sbyor ba 'di//ma tshang mar me lta bur brda//gzugs ni sangs rgyas rnam mdzad//tshor ba
56a6(rgyud): rdo rje nyi ma yin//'du shes pad ma gar dbang ste//'du byed rdo rje rgyal po yin//rnam shes rdo rje sems dpa' ste//'di dag dngos kun rang bzhin te//rdo rje mkha' 'gro zhes byar grags/yang
56a7(rgyud): dag chen po gcig pu'i lha//bde ba thams cad rab ster ba//sgyu ma chen po'i rang bzhin gyis//chags bral chags pa zhes byar bshad//sa khams gsum phyed ma yin//de bzhin chu khams
56a8(rgyud): bsod pa'o//me khams 'gugs byed ma yin te//de bzhin rlung gi gar dbang po//nam mkha'i khams ni sgyu ma che//rang nyid 'gro ma zhes byar bshad//sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam sbyor ba//
56b1(rgyud): mkha' 'gro ma nyid 'bar ba'i mchog//'byung lnga'i rang bzhin ni//de ni rdo rje rtse mor gnas mig gti mug rdo rjer bshad//rna ba zhes sdang rdo rjer bshad//sna ni ser sna rdo rje'i bshad//kha ni 'dod
56b2(rgyud): chags rdo rjer gsungs//lus ni 'phra dog rdo rje ste//skye mched thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//rdo rje mkha' 'gro bde ba'i mchog//gzugs 'di thams cad stong pa nyid//stong pa'i tshul gyi mig 
56b3(rgyud): gis ni//dogs med par sgom pa'i mchog//rnam par mi rtog pa'i lta ba//nam mkha'i tshul gyi sems kyis ni//de ni thams cad nam mkha'i dngos//dgos med pa'i sgom par ldan//
56b4(rgyud): rnam par mi rtog skye 'gyur zhes//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi
56b5(rgyud): thogs pa med cing //gdon mi bra ba bde ba chen po dang tshig ces bya ba'i rim pa'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugs nas//'di skad ces gsungs so//ji srid
56b6(rgyud): 'khor ba'i gnas su ni//mkhas mchog 'dug par 'gyur ba'i bar de srid sems can mtshungs med don//mya ngan mi mnga' bar ni mdzad//de ltar sems can dam pa rnams
56b7(rgyud): srid pa'i zhags pas 'jigs med par//mi skye ba ni brtson 'grus kyis//rtag tu sems can don rnams mdzad//nam mkha'i gnas kyi mthar thug par//smon lam rnam par 
56b8(rgyud): sbyong byed cing //sems can don brtson sems kyis ni dpa'o srid pa sbyong bar mdzad//rtag tu 'dod pas myos pa yi//lha'i bu 'di rnams dang ni//gzhan yang skye bo chags can
57a1(rgyud): @#//phyir /'dod chags thams cad dag par mdzad //bde chen shin tu grub bya'i phyir //rtogs pa rgyas par byin pa dang //thog ma tha ma med pa'i mchog //rtog pa thams cad
57a2(rgyud): dang por bshad //'das pa'i dus na sngon 'byung ba //rtog pa dang po bde ba che//der ni rdo rje sems chen gyis //lhag pa'i ni lhan cig bzhugs //'jig rten khams rnams mtha'
57a3(rgyud): yas kyi;sems can kun don 'byung ba'i phyir //glu dang rol mo sogs pa'i gar//srid pa kun gyi rtso bor 'gyur //sems can thams cad 'byung ba yi//dga' ba yang dag bsdus
57a4(rgyud): pa'i ngang //nges na sems can skye 'gyur ba'i //'dod pa'i chags pa rnam spangs te//de 'dul bar ni mkhyen nas su //yi ge las 'byung rnam spangs shing //namakha' lta bur bdag med pa //
57a5(rgyud): dag pa nyid du de nas son //de nas sems can sangs rgyas kyi /bla med don ni nus med pa'i//stong pa nyid la rtags bzhugs pas //'gro kun stong pa bcom nas ni //thams cad dmyal
57a6(rgyud): bar ltung mthong ste//slar yang bdag dang gzhan don la //dngos pas gus pa nyid du 'gyur //de nas 'jig rten khams kun la //bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas kun 'dus te//myur
57a7(rgyud): ba nyid du bdag la skul //oM ma ha su kha badzra swatA dza hUM baM ho//su ra ta stom //'dis ni skul ba gyis kyang //dag pa'i bde ba kun tu rtor;'dod pa'i don kun bsgrub byed cing //'gro kun bde ba
57a8(rgyud): skyed par 'gyur//nga ni 'dod pa'i gzugs kyi mchog //sangs rgyas kun gyis byin rlabs pa /nga nyid chos kyi sku'i mchog //sangs rgyas kunagyi bdag po yin//sangs rgyas kun gyi
57b1(rgyud): sbyor 'phrog cing //sangs rgyas kun gyi bde ba che//sangs rgyas kun gyi sku che bo //sangs rgyas kun gyi gsung snyan pa //sangs rgyas kun gyi thugs chen yin//sangs rgyas kun gyi rgyal po
57b2(rgyud): che//rdo rje mkha' 'gro 'dzin pa kun gyi bdag//'jig rten dbang phyug kun gyi rtso//rin ren 'dzin pa kun gyi dbang//mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor bas //sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam par
57b3(rgyud): 'gyur//sangs rgyas kun dang ma nyam pas na//rdo rje mkha' 'gro zhes byar grub//dkyil 'khor chen po'i sbyor ba yis //de nas dkyil 'khor thams cad bri//de'i dbus su tshul bzhin du//
57b4(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa' gzhag par bya//zla ba'i mdog can 'jum dang ldan//'od zer dmar po 'khrugs pa la//sangs rgyas phreng ba mdzes pa yis//rgyan kun gyi ni rnam par glugs //zhal
57b5(rgyud): bzhi phyag bzhi spyan gsum dang //ral pa'i thor tshugs kyis brgyan cing //lha mo bzhi dang yang dag ldan//sangs rgyas gzugs kyis nye bar brgyan//rtsa zhal zhi zhing g.yas
57b6(rgyud): dkar min//de bzhin gzhan yang g.yon pa dmar//rgyab zhal ljang ser gser bsregs 'dra//thod pa la bzhugs gar dang ldan//sum bcu gsum la 'jigs par mdzad//
57b7(rgyud): rdo rje dang ni ral gri dang /dril bu thod pa snams pa nyid //mkha' 'gro ma'i 'khor los gang //skyob pa sna tshogs bri bar bya//mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba /rdo rje mkha' 'gro bde mchog
57b8(rgyud): zhes//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs//ma dang sring mo bu mo dang //de bzhin nye du mo rnams ni//dngos grub thams cad rab bsgrubs pas
58a1(rgyud): @#//'dod pa'i sbyor bas chags par bya//ba glang sha dang rta sha sogs//dogs pa med par bza' bya//'jig rten las rtag g.yon la spyod//bstan pa dang ni g.yo ba yi//'gro kun g.yon
58a2(rgyud): las byung bas na//g.yon gyis bza' zhing g.yon mchod bya//phyog ma rtogs gzhan rnams la//'dod pa rmongs par bgrod mi bya//pho nya ma rtogs zhen thar par//'gyur ba nyid du the tsom med//
58a3(rgyud): bsgrub pos mchog tu 'bad byas te//rtul shugs mi spang rtul shugs rdzogs//dam tshig rigs pas spyad pa dang //tsa ru'i bza' ba lhan cig bya//rigs lnga ni kun spyod pa//rigs gcig
58a4(rgyud): nyid du rtag par bya//sbra rtsi khrag dang ga bur bcas//tsan dan dmar po sbyar byas la//rdo rje chu dang sres pa ni//dngos grub thams cad bsgrub mdzad pa'i//de dag bdud rtsi lngar ni 'gyur
58a5(rgyud): tho bong srin lag sbyar ba yis//rnal 'byor rig pas rtag tu bza'//zhi ba'i btung ba bzhin myangs na//dngos grub rtag pa thob par 'gyur//'di dag dam tshig dam pa ste//mdzes
58a6(rgyud): pa'i gdong can khyod la bshad//las rnams kun la thabs bca' zhing //thogs pa med par gang spyod pa/rnam par mi rtog sgom pa ni//rtul bzhugs rnams kyi mchog gi mchog/las rnaMs
58a7(rgyud): kun la kun du ni//rnam par mi rtog sgom byed cing //dogs pa med par kun du spyod//de ni dka' thub rnams kyi mchog//the tsom med pa'i sems kyis ni//skra laM la sogs rjes dngos kyi//
58a8(rgyud): yul rnams kyi mag gi ma lus ston pa yi//dka' thub shin tu mda' dka'//gang yang las rnams thams cad la//shes rab dang ni sbyar byas te//de yang stong pa'i gnas su sbyar/
58b1(rgyud): deng 'dir bdag chen dka' thub yin//rnam par mi rtog sems kyis ni//shes rab dang ni mtshungs gzugs la//dgos med spyod pa'i kun spyod la//de ni bdag chen dka' thub yin //shes rab thabs ni ma rtogs par
58b2(rgyud): gang tshe sems gzhan mi g.yon//de tshe byang chub chen po ni//nges par brtan pa nyid du bshad//rnam par mi rtog rjes 'jug la//rnal 'byor pa'i mngon par brtson//de ni thams cad 'byung ba yin//
58b3(rgyud): shes rab pha rol phyin pa yin//gang tshe 'dus byas tshul rnams ni//rnam par mi rtog semasakyis ni//me long lta bur rab shes pa//de ni shes rab brtan pa yin//glog dang chu bur ltar smin
58b4(rgyud): pa//gang tshe 'dus byas tshularnams ni//gang gis ji ltar mthong gyur pa//de ni shes rab bstan pa yin//me long nang gi gzugs rnyan dang //rmi lam sgyu ma dri za'i grong //mig
58b5(rgyud): 'phrul mtshungs pa gang gis mthong //'bras bu khyad 'phags thob 'gyur pa//ci'i phyir na 'dod ma bya//brtan g.yo dngos po yod yin te//'on kyang snang ba tsam mtshan nyid//sgom khyad par
58b6(rgyud): tsam zhig//goms pa'i stobs ni rdzogs pa ni yid 'ong pa'i 'bras bu ni//bla med gsal ba'i bla'i mthu//'bras bu don gnyer rgyu bsgrubs nas//der ni lha mo su zhig 'gog//de bas 'bad pas thaMs
58b7(rgyud): cad kyis//kun tu 'gro ma mchod par bya//dka' thub sdom pa mi zad pas//lus ni skams shing sdug bsngal la//sdug bsngal gyis ni sems g.yengs nas//g.yengs pas dngos grub gzhan du
58b8(rgyud): 'gyur//lus dang sems ni brtan 'gyur//bde ba thams cad brtan par 'gyur//sdug bsngal gyis ni g.yengs gyur pas//shin tu 'gog par 'gro bar 'gyur//des ni lus dang ngag seMs
59a1(rgyud): @#//rnams brtan pas byas na byang chub thob//gzhan du dus min 'chi ba dang //dmyal bar nges par sreg 'gyur zhes//mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor bas //rdo rje mkha' 'gro bde ba'i
59a2(rgyud): mchog//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs/bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs //rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro las //de kho na nyid snang ba dam pa'i yul la 'jug
59a3(rgyud): pa'i ye shes te//rim par phye ba dang po'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das la btsun mo rnams kyis slar yang mchod cing //phyag byas nas 'di skad gsol to//bcom ldan 'das
59a4(rgyud): snying po gang gis de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis bstan pa 'bral ba'i lha la sogs pa'i bdug pa can rnams dang //skye bo bdug pa can gzhan dag kyang 'dul ba dang //'jos
59a5(rgyud): pa dang zhum par byed pa dang //ltung ba dang bzhi bar byas te//byang chub kyi snying po'i bar du gnod par byed par mi 'gyur ba'i snying po //sdong ba thams cad rab tu 'joms
59a6(rgyud): pa//lhas bshad du gsol//de nas bcom ldan 'da'as de bzhin gshegs ba thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje'i bdag pos glur blangs te 'di skad ces gsungs so//dang 
59a7(rgyud): por rnam snang mdzad pa gnas pa//der ni sde tshan rnams kyi mtha'//rta dang ba'i d+ha ba'i mtha'//dbyangs kyi gsum pa'i spyi brgyan pa'i //sa'i mtha' ni rang gis lta //la sa'i dbus kyi 
59a8(rgyud): gdan bzhugs pa //sa'i mtha' ni sbyar bar bya//mda' gnyis kyis ni rnam par sbyangs//a bstan gnas pa sa'i mtha'//slar yang sa mtha' mda' lnga stan//zla ba'i dum bu theg les
59b1(rgyud): brgyan//la sa'i dbus su sa bon ni //mda' cu yis ni yongasu sbyangs //sa dang sa'i dbus gnas pa//zhal gsum gyis ni brgyan pa dang //rlung gis phyogs nas sa bon la //ru tra yis

61a3(rgyud): ba'i sgra la ni //thod pa yis ni dbu brgyan bcings//ro langs rnams dang lhan cig rol//he ru ka'i gar gyis mchod//ma mo'i grong khyer rnams dga' bar //sa'i dbang phyug rol par mdzad/

62a1(rgyud): @#//rdo rje mkha' 'gro las /las thams cad kyi cho ga las sgyu ma chen po rnam par 'phrul pa'i sngags kyi cho ga'i rim par phye ba ste gnyis pa'o////ud ta te /b+ha bad si
