93b5(rgyud): gangs dang 'dra bar 'dzag pa ste//mchog tu zhi ba'i ha la ni//dbyangs yig drug pa dang sbyar bas//thig le steng du brgyan pa yi//yi ge'i bkod pa dgod par bya//rnal 'byor ba'i dbugs rdugs byed//dngos dang dngos
93b6(rgyud): med rnams spangs pa//rang gi 'grin pa dbus mar yang//pad ma dkar po kun 'od mdzes//der ni thig le bzla ba yis//brgyad pa'i dbyangs yig bcu gsum pa//bu lu ta zhes bya bar grags//mgo bo klad pa'i dbus gnas
93b7(rgyud): par//thig le gzugs can mi shigs pa'o//a ta sa'i me tog bzhin//'og tu ltas shing 'chang ba ste//nyin dang mtshan du bdud rtsi yi//tshul gyis 'bab pas yang dag gnas//dang po 'khor lo sgoms nas ni//dbyangs
93b8(rgyud): yig gis ni khyad par sbyar//rang gi phyag rgyar ston ldan pas//sangs rgyas byang chub rab tu ster//de'i rtul shugs ma bshad de//sbyin sreg las dang sngags rnams dang bzung ba dang ni bgrang phreng dag//
94a1(rgyud): @#//gcig nas gcig tu lnga go par 'gyur//sngags kyi dkyil 'khor dbyibs chos dag //sbyor ba rnam gsum zhes bya ste//dug med byas nas nan tan du//sten bya 'di ni sten bya ba//srid pa'i longaspyod dag
94a2(rgyud): 'gyur zhing //srid pas srid mchog gi bdag //ye shes rnam pa gsum gyis ni//srid pa'i nyes pas gos mi 'gyur//rnal 'byor ba'i sbyor ba 'di//rtag tu rgyun du goms bya na//zla drug tsam gyis nges par ni//rdo rje sems dpa' mnyaM
94a3(rgyud): 'gyur zhes//rdo rje mkha'agro bde ba'i mchog //bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor yongs rdzogs pa//ba ka'i dbusu sgom par bya//de yi dbus 'gyur sa bon no//sA+o zhing cung zad dmar pa'i 'od//'od zer
94a4(rgyud): dmar po'i phrang 'khrugs las//'dab skyes sngon po lta bu'o//zhal cig phyag bzhi spyan gsum pa//mgor skyes kham pa gyen phreng zhing //rgyan rnams kun gyis brgyan par blubs//rkang pa sdug gi lpags bkris//
94a5(rgyud): phyag na lcags kyu zhags pa dang //mda' dang gzhu ni mchog tu 'gyengs//in tra ni la lta bu ste//dang po yi ge las byung sgom//rang gi rig pa rab 'byor pas//kun nas de ma thag tu ni//dmar po 'u ni sgoms nis ni//
94a6(rgyud): de nas cho ga bzhin du bya//dbyangs yig gnyis dang ldan par//srid pa gsum po sgom par bya//'bad pas bsgrub bya snang bya ste//slar yang de nyid du ni bzhug//des ni sa gsum ma lus par//sgrub pa pos dbang du 'gyur
94a7(rgyud): tshangs pa khyab 'jug drag pa dang //dbang po dang ni 'dod lha swogs//yi ge gis phan tshun skul ba yis//chung ma dang ni bcas te 'gugs//bcom ldan de nyid shes bya ste//gcod pa gar gyi rtso bo yin//de nas dbu ma'i
94a8(rgyud): mtha' dang ldan//thig le sgras yang dag gnas//dngos po sgom bya sgom byas nas//gong du 'gugs pa'i dam pa yin//ba ga'i ni dbus 'gyur ba//ye shes rgya mtsho sgoms byas la//yi ge mi 'gyur rdzogs pa la//
94b1(rgyud): sku mdog dkar po zhal bzhi pa//rdo rje dang ni khwa traM snams//dang 'o spyi rtsu ga zla phyed rgyan//shor dang thod pa'ang snams pa swe//dbugs mi g.yo bar sgom par bya//rlung ni me dang yang dag ldan//chu dang dbang chen mnan pa ste//
94b2(rgyud): spro ba'i bdag nyid can gyis spro//'bad de bsgrub bya dmigs nas ni//khu ba 'gugs pa'i dam pa yin//ci'i ye shes las byung ba'i sku mdog ser po kun/tu 'bur//zhal bzhi pa la phyag bzhi pa//mgo skyes thod pa'i phreng
94b3(rgyud): bas rgyan//sdig 'dzub rdo rje zhags pa dang //ka pa la ni phyag g.yon na//g.yas na 'bar ba'i rdo rje dang //de bzhin du ni lcags kyu mchog//rta zhal tshul du byas nas ni//chang dgug pa'i dam yin//ri'i ye shes las byung
94b4(rgyud): 'od zer dmar pa drag po che//dpa' bo thod pa'i phreng ba can//rgyan rnams kun gyis rnam par glubs//zhal bzhi pa la phyag bzhI ba//lce spyang gi gdong can te//khwe traM kha dang thod pa dang //rdo rje de bzhin ral gri snams//khrag
94b5(rgyud): gis gang ba'i lus can sgom//'bad de bsgrub bya dmi gas nas ni//mkha'agro ma'i 'khor lo 'phro//rdo rje phra mos phug nas su//'jig pa nyid rab sgoms nas//khrag 'bugs pa'i dam pa zhes//sgyu ma kun dang mnyam sbyor ba'i
94b6(rgyud): rdo rje 'kha'agro zhes drags pa//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs//rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje'ang kha 'gro las//sgyu ma kun du rnam par 'phrul pa'i rim par phye ba ste bcu
94b7(rgyud): gcig pa'o////rdo rje mkha'agro'i bsgrub thabs ni//bshad par byas lha mo nyon//dang por ro sgom bya zhing //mkhas pas chos dbyangs rang bzhin bsam//rnal 'byor pa ni der gnas nas/go 'phya
94b8(rgyud): mchog ni sgom par bya//rang snying zla ba'i dbusu ni//de nyid thams cad bkod nasu//de'i 'od zer las byung ba//mkha'agro ma sogs mchod nas ni//phyag byas n+sa ni 'di skad brjod//sangs rgyas byang chub seMda' rnaMs//kun+sa bdag la dgongasu+ol
95a1(rgyud): //bdag ming 'di zhes bgyi ba ni/dus 'di nas bzung nas ji srid du//byang chub snying por mchis kyi bar//ji ltar dus gsum mgon po rnams/yang dag byang chub nges mdzad pa//de bzhin
95a2(rgyud): bla med byang chub sems//dam pa bdag gis skyed par bgyi//sangs rgyas rnal 'byor skyes dom la//sangs rgyas chos dang dge 'dun ste//bla na med pa'i mchog gsum//de nas rtsams te mchog tu gzugs//
95a3(rgyud): bslab pa yi ni tshul khrims dang //dge ba'i chos ni bsdu pa dang //seMs can don byed tshul khrims gsuM//bdag gis brtan por sor sor bzung //rdo rje rigs mchog chen po la//rdo rje dril bu'i phyag rgya yang //yang dag nyid du
95a4(rgyud): bzung bar bgyi zhing //slob dpon dag kyang bdag gis bzung //rin chen rigs chen rnal 'byor gyis//dam tshig yid du 'ong ba la//nyin re bzhin du dus drug tu//sbyin pa rnam bzhi brtsal bar bgyi//byang chub chen po las byung ba
95a5(rgyud): pad ma'i rigs chen dam pa la//phyi nang gsang ba'i theg pa gsuM//dam chos yang dag bzung bar bgyi//las kyi rigs mchog chen po la //mchod pa ci nus bgyi pa la//sdom pa thams cad ldan par bgyi//bla na med pa'i byang
95a6(rgyud): chub seMs//daM pa de ni bskyed pa'i zhing //sems can kun gyi don gyi phyir//sdom pa ma lus bzung bar bgyi//ma sgral ba rnams sgral ba dang //ma grol ba rnaM bdag gis dgrol//dbugs ma dbyung ba dbugs phyung zhing /'gro kun
95a7(rgyud): mya ngan las bkod//de nas oM swa b+ha ba shud+ha sarbA d+harma swa b+ha ba shud+ha hA/de nas tshig 'di rjod nas chos thaMd bdag med par lta ste/de nyid bco ldan 'das rdo rje can//rdo rje seMs dpa' de bzhin gshegs//sku mdog sngon po 'jig chen bo//
95a8(rgyud): thod pa'i phreng bas rnam par glubs//zhal bzhi pa la phyag bzhi pa//spyan gsuM gyis ni mdzes pa ste//yid kyis chen po gdan du bya//nyi ma la bzhugs gar dang ldan//phyag na thod pa khwa traM kha//de bzhin mda' bzhu snaMs pa'o//
95b1(rgyud): cha ga'i bya ba de bzhin nyid//ye shes rgya mtsho sgom par bya//shar ni dkar zhing 'od sngo ste //g.yon na khwa taM dril bu dang //g.yas na rdo rje thod pa'o//byang phyogs ma rgad ser skya'i 'od//g.yon na khwa traM zhags pa dang //g.yas na rde chung
95b2(rgyud): thod pa snams//glang chen lce spyang mi dang ni//glang gi thod pa rims bzhin bzhugs//dang por ltung byed ma yin te//gnyis pa de bzhin bsod ma yin//gsum la 'gugs ma zhes bya ste/bzhi pa gar gyi dbang phyug ma//
95b3(rgyud): rig ma de dag gyen skrag 'bar//brgyan rnams thams cad rnam par grubs//gar dang ldan pas yang dag bzhugs//phyogs dag ni bzhi po ru//thod pa rnam pa bzhi po bkod//sangs rgyas bzugs kyis rnam par
95b4(rgyud): brgyan//spyi rtsugs la bar yang dag ldan//dang po yi ge ldan pa ru//sgos na skad cig de la 'bar/lha mo de ltar sgoms na ni/rdzas kyi dngos grub 'bras rab ster//sbyor ba'i dngos rig pa ste//yid
95b5(rgyud): ches yang dag 'jug par 'gyur//mi snang ba dang thaMs cad dang //sbyor ba skad cig gis mthong 'gyur//lha mchog la sogs 'phrogs nasu//sangs rgyas kun gyi bu mo khyod//gang gi ji snyed sangs
95b6(rgyud): rgyas rnams//nyid kyi rdo rje gdan bzhugs pa//lag du shing thog gzhag pa bzhin//rdo rje mig gis mthong bar 'gyur//gang gi ji snyed zhing khams na//sku gsung thugs kyi mtshan nyid rnams//thams cad rang gi seMs
95b7(rgyud): nyid kyi//rol mo las byung mthong bar 'gyur//sngags kyi rdo rje mtshan yi ge//rgyud dang sngags ni sgom par bya//gsang chen dam pa de'i ni//mdzes ma khyod la bshad pa yin//tho rangs rang gi snying kar ni//a las
95b8(rgyud): zla ba'i dkyil 'khor bsam//de stengs yi ge hUM bsgoms la//byams pa'i sems ni bskyed nas su//de nas dkyil 'khor bri bar bya//gru bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa//kha khyer de bzhin gshegs pas mtshan//skud pa
96a1(rgyud): @#//bzhi dang yang dag ldan//dar kyi phreng bas mdzes par byas //dkyil 'khor grwa rnams thams cad dang //sgo dang sgo khyud 'tshams rnamasu //rdo rje rin chen rnam spras la //phyau'i dkyil 'khor thig btab
96a2(rgyud): bya//shes rab can gyis de nang du//'khor lo phreng bas rnam brgyan te //de'i dbus 'gyur pad ma ni //dkar po shin du mdzes par bya//slar yang de'i dbus 'gyur bas //'do ldan pad ma dmar po bri //de nas sngags
96a3(rgyud): pas de nang du//rdo rje phreng bas kun 'khrigs bri//mgro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba'i //rdo rje mkha' 'gro bde ba'i mchog//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//bcom ldan 'das rdo rje can gyis gsungs//rgyud kyi rgyal po
96a4(rgyud): chen po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro las //rdo rje 'kha' 'gro 'byung ba'i rim par phye ba ste bcu gnyis pa'o////de nas gzhan yang bshad bya ba /dngos grub thams cad rab bsgrub pa //gang zhig shes pa tsam gyis ni /
96a5(rgyud): bsgrubs pas dngos grub 'gyur pa'o//a ni sna rtser bsaMs nasu//ba ni ling ga'i lte bar bsam de nas mnyam par sbyar byas te /rnal 'byor ma'i 'khor lo kun pad ma'i lte ba rnamasu sbyar//a ba d+ho ta'i
96a6(rgyud): gnas de bzhin smin ma'i bar der gzhag par bya//dbyings yig dang por yang dag ldan//sgoms na skad cig de la 'bar//de nas 'bar phreng rgya mtshor ni//kun nas sgo'i 'khor lor bsam //lte ba snying
96a7(rgyud): ka la dang ni //phral stengs spyi rtsug 'khor lo ru//dang po sngags tshogs byin gyis rlabs //lte ba snying ka rnal 'byor ma//lce gnyis dpa' bo byin gyis rlabs//dpa' bo dang ni rnal 'byor ma//gnyis med cho ga ji bzhin du //
96a8(rgyud): 'gal me 'khor lo la gnas pa//mkha' 'gro ma ni de bzhin 'khor //klog lta bu ni gsal ba'i 'od//yid shugs chen pos rig pa che //lha'i rnal 'byor dusu ni //phyogs bcu rnaMsu spro bar bya//shin du phra ba'i rdul tshogs ni//shin du phra ba'i
96b1(rgyud): rdul tshogs ni //shin du phra ba'i dngos por sgom //'gyur ba med cing snang ba med //kun mkhyen 'bras bu ster ba'o //me tog la ni gnas bsams te //gang la byin te rgyas 'debs 'gyur //dung dang zla 'dra'i 'od mdzes pa //ltung
96b2(rgyud): byed ma'i sbyor bas bsam //sgrubs bya kha ru bcug nas ni //lhan cig zlas nas ltung bar 'gyur //de nas nyi ma la gnas pI //gdung tshul nyi ma'i 'od dang ni //bskal pI me 'bar rab 'jigs pa'i //bsod ma bsaMs te yid kyis ni /
96b3(rgyud): bsgrub bya lhan cig gnas gyur nas //steng du ltas na 'chi bar 'gyur //de nas rlung dang me dang ni //dkyil 'khor la gnas me stag gi //phung po 'phro ba'i 'gug+sa ma bsaM //y+di kyis bsgrub bya'i snying zhugs te //de'i snying ni rtsa nas
96b4(rgyud): phyung //'gugs ma'i snying zlas 'gug+sa par 'gyur //rlung gi dkyil 'khor seMs kyis ni/rlung gi dkyil 'khor la gnate //rlung mdog sgrub bya rlung nang bsaM //slar yang bdag dang bsgrub bya ni //gcig tu 'gyur te gar mdzad pI
96b5(rgyud): sngags zlos ci bzhin gang byas nas //gar byed dusu 'jug par 'gyur //mchod pa rdo rje rab sbyor gyis //de nas las r+n+saM kun bsgrub bya //snying kha nyid dang de bzhin kha //mgo dang spyi zug mtshon bya ste //lte ba snying ka r+n+sasu ni
96b6(rgyud): dbugs mi g.yo bas sgom par bya //dang po rdo seMs dpA ste //gnyis pa pad ma gar dbang rgyal //gsuM pa rnaM pa snang mdzad de //de bzhin bzhi ba rta mchog rgyal //lnga pa rdo rje nyi ma ste //drug pa he ru kar ni brjod //slar y+nga lte ba snying ka dang
96b7(rgyud): de bzhin kha dang mgo dang ni //spyi chu ga matsh+na cha d+da dig ste //rdo rje phag mo gshin rje ma //de bzhin rmongs ma skyong ma ste //skrag ma dang ni rtumo dang //drug po 'di dag rig ma che //pha rol phyin pa rnaM pa drug //de nas dbang bskur bsgoms nas ni //phyi n+sa
96b8(rgyud): mchod pa rtsam par bya bdag nyid he ru kar byas nas //dpaldan he ru kar dran bya //daM tshig ye shes gcig gyur pas //der 'dir rdo rje can du brjod //rnal 'byor 'di ni mach+do che na dang //de bzhin sngags kyi bkod pa ste //de nas dpa' ba mach+do shes nas //las ni
97a1(rgyud): @#//'bad pa nyid kyis bya//sngags pas rang dgar dbye bya ste//phyin ci log tu nges par sbyar//tshar gcod pa dang phan 'dogs pa'i//las kyi mdog tu rnam par sgo//de nyid dbye bas phye nas
97a2(rgyud): ni//rtsa dang lam tu phye nas ni//rtsa dang lam du sbyar bya//sbyo ba yi ni don rnams la//dga' ba chen pos 'di sgrub bya//cho ga 'di yis bsgrubs nas ni//lha dang lha min mir bcas 'grub//
97a3(rgyud): 'kha'agro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba'i//rdo rje mkha'agro bde ba'i mchog//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs//rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje
97a4(rgyud): mkha'agro las//dpa' bo gnyis su med pa'i mchod pa dang //las kyi tshogs rab tu byung ba'i rim par phye ba ste//cu gsuM pa'o////de nas sku gsung thugs 
97a5(rgyud): kyis ni//'khor lor gnas pa'i bye brag dang //gnas kyi rab tu dbye ba dang //yul gyi dbye ba bshad par bya//mgo ni ma la swa yul te//spyi rtsug dza lan da ra yin//u rgyan 
97a6(rgyud): rna ba g.yas pa yin//ar bu ta ni rgyab tshogs yin//gnas kyi ming can rna bzhi'o//go da ba ri rna ba g.yon//ra ma sha ri smin dbus gsungs//mgo ni de ba sku tri ste//dpung pa ma
97a7(rgyud): la ya zhes brjod//'di dag nye ba'i gnas yin te//gnas kyi khyad par tshul gyis ni//mkha'agro thugs kyi 'khor lor gnas//ka ma ru mtshan khung de bzhin du nu A+oM ter rab tu grag+sa
97a8(rgyud): de dag zhing ste lte ba ni//tri shu ku ni zhes bya ba'i//sna'i rtse mor ko sa la//de dag nye ba'i zhing du brjod//kha ni ka ling ka yin te//mgrin pa laM pa ka ru brjod//tshan do ha zhes bya bar grag+sa/
97b1(rgyud): mtshan ni yi dgas pa'i ste//'gri ha de ba dang bshang laM/de dag 'du sar brjod pa ste//rla ni so ra s+t+ra zhes bya//'byin pa gnyis ni gser ling ste//nye ba'i 'dun pa nyid yino//sor mo rnams ni na ga ra/
97b2(rgyud): snying kha ka ka ni tsi zhesu grags //chu so hi ma lam ya zhes bya//nye ba'i rol par brjod pa yin/gnas kyi khyad par tshul gyis ni//sa spyod gsung gi 'khor lor gnas //rkang ba'i bol ni du ste/
97b3(rgyud): dur khrod ces ni bya bar brjod //the bong ma ru zhes bya ste//pus mo ku lu ta zhes brjod //nye ba'i dur khrod sa 'og ni ni/sku'i 'khor lor yang dag gnas/rang lus skyes pa 'di dag ni /
97b4(rgyud): mkha'agro ma'i gnas su brjod //phyi dang nang dang yang dag gnas//nyi shu rtsa bzhir phye ba yis//sku gsung thugs kyi 'khor lo yin//gnas rnaMs dag ni thaMs cad du/rtsa rnaMs kun ni yang
97b5(rgyud): dag bshad/yul 'di rnaMs ni bu mo gang //rtsa'i gzugs kyis gnas zhes //dpa' bo kun dang mnyam sbyor ba'i/'kha'agro ma'i dra ba sdom//rdo rje sems de bzhin gshegs//
97b6(rgyud): bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs//slar yang bsdus te bshad bya ba /deni bdag gis bshad kyis nyon //pu la ri ma la yar thod pa'i dum bu can dang /rab tu rtum po ste//so
97b7(rgyud): dang sen mo 'bab pa ste/dza lan da rar keng ru a chen po dang /rtuM mig ma ste/sgra dang spu 'bab pa'o//o ya nar keng dang 'od ldan ma ste/pags pa dang //dri ma 'bab pa'o//ar bur
97b8(rgyud): dang mche ba rnaM par rtsigs pa dang //sna chen ma ste//sha 'bab pa ste gnas pa'o//go da ba yar lha dang dpa' bo'i slob ste//thams cad du rgyus pa 'bab pa'o//ra me sha re rar da 'od dpag med du
98a1(rgyud): @#//med pa dang me'u thung ma ste/rus pa'i 'phreng ba 'bab pa'o//de ba'i ko ter rdo rje 'od dang /lang ga'i dbang phyug ma ste//rtag tu 'chin pa 'bab pa'o//ma la ber rdo rje lus dang ljon grib ma ste/
98a2(rgyud): snying khrug 'bab pa ste nye ba'i gnaso//de ltar bstan pa 'di dag ni //thugs kyi 'khor lo mkhas spyod do//snyoms par bzhugs pas yang dag gnas //ka ma la ru par ni nyu gu can dang //sa
98a3(rgyud): srungs ma ste//rtag tu mig 'bab pa'o//o ter ni rdo rje'i ral pa can dang 'jigs byed chen po ste/'khris pa 'bab par grags pa ste//zhing ngo/hri sha ku ner dpa'o chen po dang //rlung gi shugs

98a6(rgyud): la yar ni mig mi bzang dang kha gdong ma ste/'tshogs ma'i srubs 'bas pa ste//nye ba'i rol sa'o//gsung gi 'khor lo sa spyod do//snyoms par bzhugs pas yang dag gnas /tre ta pa rir

98b1(rgyud): dgra dang shin du dpa' mo ste/tshil 'bab pa'o//sin du'i yul du ni pad ma gar gyi dbang phyug dang /stobs chen ma ste//rtag tu 'chi ma 'bab pa'o//ma rung ni rnaM par snang mdzad dang 'khor lo sgyur ma
98b2(rgyud): ste 'chil ma 'bab par grags pa'o//ku lu tar ni rdo rje sems dpa' dang brtson 'grus chen mo ste//sna chu 'bab pa ste/nye ba'i dur khrod do//sku ri 'khor lo sa 'og spyod//snyoms par zhugs pas
98b3(rgyud): zhugs pa yin/zhes mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba'i//rdo rje mkha' 'gro bde ba'i mchog//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs//rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po
98b4(rgyud): dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro las//dpa' bo gnyisu med pa'i gnas rtan pa zhes bya ba'i rim par phye ba ste bcu bzhi pa'o////de nas kyi rab mchog ni//sbyor ba kun gyi dam pa ste//gang yang 'grub
98b5(rgyud): par byed pI sngags//de'i dbye ba rim ma bzhin bshad//yi ge pho nya la sogs sngags//mda'i shugs kyis yongasu 'khor//sprul pa'i 'khor lo dbus su ni/sde tshan brgyad kyis shin du skor//
98b6(rgyud): sngags rnaMs thams cad 'byung ba ste//gzhan yang cir yung rung ba bskyed//chos kyi 'khor lo zhes bya ba//lus can rnams kyi snying dga' byed//sgra lnga dang ni yang dag ldan//yang la ba rnam par
98b7(rgyud): klubs longaspyod 'khor lo zhes bya ba//ro rnams kyi ni rten rang bzhin//cha rnaMs bzhi po dag gis ni//kun nas yongs su skor ba'o//bde ba chen po'i 'khor lo che//byang chub sum cu gnyis dang ldan/
98b8(rgyud): zla ba nyi ma zhes bya ba//g.yon dang g.yas kyi ngos gnyisu//dpa'o mkha' 'gro ma rnaMs kyis//'gro dang 'ong bar nges par brjod//gnas gnyis la ni mnyam par zhon/'og dang steng du yang dag rten//
99a1(rgyud): @#//g.yon dang g.yas kyi ngos gnyisu//dbyangs yig bcu gnyis steng ltas bri//ka stsogs 'og tu ltas pa ni//ngos gnyis dag tu ltas pa ste/dbus su der ni sbyar bar bya/de ltar
99a2(rgyud): bstan pa 'di dag ni//rtsa dang sgra'i rang bzhin no//gnyis med ye shes rdo rje ste/nam mkha'i sgra'i rang bzhin no//nges 'byung sangs rgyas thaMs cad te//gang du skye ba zhes byar grags//shin tu rnam dag ye shes che//lha mo kun gyi
99a3(rgyud): rang bzhin gang //rdo rje sems dpa' zhes bya ste//mchog tu bde ba brjod pa yin/rang 'byung gi ni gzugs 'di nyid//spro dang sdud par byed pa'o//yig pho nya la sogs sngags//der ni che dang bral ba gang //bka'i dbusu 
99a4(rgyud): gzugs med par//der ni rtag tu gnas pa yin//sprin dbus pad ma'i zla dbus su//bcu drug skad pas skor ba yin/shes rab thabs kyi mnyam sbyor ba'i//kha sbyar zhes byar brjod pa yin//lag gnyis kun tu
99a5(rgyud): 'khyud pa ni//rdo rje lu gu rgyud bcings brjod//slar yang rnam par grol ba'i laM//'bab pa'i tshogs ni mchog tu dga'//sbyor ba kha sbyor nyid dang ni//rdo rje lu gu rgyud du gzhed/rdo rje pad mas dbye ba dang 
99a6(rgyud): sgo chung sbrang rtsi byed par bshad//skor ba gang phyir skyod ba ste//bde ba'i thabs kyis kun rig pa'o//dga' dang bde ba'i rang bzhin gyis//zhabs pad mor bcug pa yin//chags pa lcags khyu kho na ste/de
99a7(rgyud): bzhin gshegs pa thaMs cad dgug//mi 'khrug rdo rjes dkrus par bya//de ni rtun shing zhes byar brjod//de nas chags stsogs nyon mongs kyi bag chags dum bu nyid du bya//shin tu rgya cher dmigs pa yin/
99a8(rgyud): kun rdzob de bzhin btung ba dang /dngos grub rdo rje min par bya//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//ci ltar bas na 'gyur de dbus su/rtag gdong 'dzin dang bstud pa'o//de ni de nyid ces bya ste/rang 'byung gcig
99b1(rgyud): gis khyab pa'o//gang ming sangs rgyas thaMs cad kyis//sgyu ma zhes byar grags pa ste//gsang ba mchog gis dges pas na//thaMs cad bdag nyid gang gnas pa'o//'di ni kun mkhyen zhes bya ste//kun tu 'gro ba mi shig+sa
99b2(rgyud): pa'o//pad ma yi ni sna rtse ru /rgyu dang mi rgyu kun sgo bya//phyi rol yige spangs nasu /yid kyis zla dang ldan par bya//nges pa'i go 'phang rtag tu gnas//brtan pa tsha dbyings rang bzhin no//rtag tu phral ba rtul
99b3(rgyud): dang bral//ye shes sku ste dri ma med//gsal ba chos kyi rang bzhin shes /myangan 'das pa 'ga' zhig yul //phan tshun 'dud pa'i gsang sngags ni //bye brag tu ni phye nasu//sa bon phreng bas de nas sgo
99b4(rgyud): hUM gi skud pa rgyu bar bya //sgrub bya 'gog par gyur na ni//gom pa gcig kyang mi 'gro'o//'di yi shes rab lhan skyes te/de'i gzug+sa su gyur par gnas//las kyi rlung gis skyod nasu //lte ba'i dkyil
99b5(rgyud): 'khor 'di nas 'bar //sgyu ma chen mo zhes byar bshad//las rnams thaMs cad rab bsgrub pa'o//skra rtse rgyar gyur pa'i tshad //skad cig klogi 'od zer can//lha'i rnal 'byor dus su ni/ba spu rnams kyi bu ga
99b6(rgyud): nas;phyogs cu rnamasu spros nas ni //lha dang lha'i man kun sdigs te//snying ka chos kyi 'khor lo ni //bsregs te longs spyod 'khor lor song //sna khung g.yasu byung nas ni//slar yang zhugs te sman
99b7(rgyud): 'tshamasu /mdzod spu'i khung nas phyung nasu /phyogs bcur bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas dang /byang chub sems dpa' thaMs cad kyi //sna sgo g.yon du zhugs nas ni //spyi bo'i 'khor lo bsregs nas su
99b8(rgyud): sngon gyi laM nas slar byung ste//slar yang spyi rtsug tu ni bzhugs//sregs par 'gyur ba'i sangs rgyas rnaMs //dga' ba bskyed nas lte ba yi //dkyil 'khor du ni 'ongs gyur nas//sngon bzhin du ni gnas
100a1(rgyud): @#//par 'gyur//dus min 'chi ba las rgyal nyid//yan lag kha traM kha dang sbyar//thod pa'i mchod yon snod du ni//zhal du mchod yon nges sbyar zhes//mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba'i//rdo rje mkha' 'gro 
100a2(rgyud): bde ba'i mchog//rdo rje ses dpa' de bzhin gshegs//bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs //rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro las//sku gsuM dang sngags dang /rgyud kyi rgya 'byung ba dang /'chi ba slu ba zhes
100a3(rgyud): bya ba'i le'u rim par phye ba ste bco lnga pa'o////de nas dngos grub kun gyi gnas /gsang ba mchog gi dges pa ste//yid 'ong bcud ni kun gyi rten//sems can kun la bde ster ba/'dod pa'i yon
100a4(rgyud): tan lnga yis rgyan//bla re dam pa bres pas 'bar/'dab stong ldan pa'i bde ma che//sbyor ba'i gdan la zhugs pa la//der ni lha mos zhus gyur pa//'khor lo kun gyi nges pa dang //dbyangs
100a5(rgyud): rnams kyi ni bye brag dang //yi ge dbye ba ma 'tshal te//'khor lo'i grangs ni ci lta bu /de rnams las ni ci zhig lags//sna tshogs klu'i sngags kyis ni//'khrul 'khor dag ni mdor bsdus
100a6(rgyud): dang /'khor lo dbang phyug dus su ni/de bzhin dpa'o kyis stan//'di dag lin the tsom mchis//bde ba chen pos bshad du gsalo//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//rgyud rnams kun tu sbas pa dang 
100a7(rgyud): de dag thams cad gzhu 'dod pa/legaso legaso lha mo che//blo gros chen mo legaso legs//'kha' 'gro ma ni thams cad kyang //the tsom skyes na gzhan ci smos//sgyu ma kun dang rnaM 'phrul dang //nyon cig lha
100a8(rgyud): mo sgyu ma che//gang zhig bris pa tsam gyis ni//bsgrub pos dngos grub thob 'gyur pa//mdor bsdus nas ni//brjod bya ste//de ni bdag gis bshad kyis nyon//nag dang dmar dang rtag tu gyur nas//
100b1(rgyud): khyad par du ni rtor ma sbyin//tsa ru'i bza' ba lhan cig bya/bsgrub pa pos yis yongasu skor//'di yis sgrub po ku la ni//ma mo dga' 'gyur the tsom med//de nas mkha' 'gro ma rnams ni//sa rnams la
100b2(rgyud): ni rnam par rol//brtson chen 'khor lo sgyu ma dang /stobs chen mo dang rab dpa' mo//'khor lo'i cho ga glang sna ma khan da ro ha 'khor lo'i shugs//bya gdong ma dang rta sna mo//shin tu bzang
100b3(rgyud): mo sngo sangs mo//chad mthung ma dang rlung shugs ma //de bzhin 'jigs byed chen mo dang //sa srungs ma dang ljon grib ma/lang ka'i dbang po mi thub mo//dpa' mo blo ma snang chen ma//'od ldan
100b4(rgyud): ma dang rtum mig ma//rab tu ma ste/grub byed yin/mkha'agro ma ni nyi shu bzhi//'di dag sngon nyid grub pa nyid//rang ming brjod pa'i sngags dang ni/mthar ni hU~M hU~M dang sbyar/oM ni thos cad gsal
100b5(rgyud): byed do//grub rtsol 'dod pa thos cad la/rnal 'byor dam mchog 'di mthong na//gzhan dag snying po med rtsar mtshungs//dpa' bo gnyis 'gyur grub pa ni/dpal ldan he ru ka'i ni
100b6(rgyud): dkyil 'khor dam pa mthong ba yin//de nas de shes rtag sgom pa//dngos grub de bzhin gshegs//pas gsungs//nyon cig lha mo shes rab can/rig pa'i rgyal po bsgrub pa'i
100b7(rgyud): thabs//'khor lo//chen po rmad gyur pa//gdengs ka'i dbang phyug zhes bya ste//thos pa tsam gyis bsreg byed na//ming dang ldan par bris yis//gdengs ka thams cad smos ci dgos//
100b8(rgyud): dbang ldan nas ni rtsams nasu//klu rnams sa bon las byung ba//rin chen dang ldan gdengs gsuM thod//gzhan yang phyogs ni kun du bas//de nas mgur ma 'di zhes te//shar du k+Sha maM bar du
101a1(rgyud): @#//brid nub tu hri ste byang du haM/gshin rje hU~M zhes bya bri//ki li ki li yi /dgas 'dud srin por tsa la ji snyed pa//rlung du si li si li bri//me'i phyogasu 'di nyid de /dang po rab rtum ma nas ni//brtson chen ma nas mthar
101a2(rgyud): thug par //sngags 'di dag ni byin gyis rlabs//'khor lo 'di ni skal chen ldan //dgra rnams kyi ni gnod spyod pa//hU~M phaT zhes bya spel ba yi//bsgrub bya de'i dbus su ni//lan gcig zlas pa'i gu gul gyis
101a3(rgyud): bdug pa nyid du sbyar byas la//klu rnams thams cad 'babs par 'gyur//lag pa g.yon pa la 'gyor lo//'di dngos su 'am yid kyis bris la//sngags lan cig zlas pa byas na/'chi bas dus su
101a4(rgyud): zin pa slong bar byed do//ka ra bi ra'i smyug gus bris la//lan cig bzlas pas reg na/mngal du gang 'dod pa der 'pho bar 'gyuro//dug dang lan tsha dang /skye tses bris te 'khros na me bsod
101a5(rgyud): par 'gyuro//ro bsregs pa'i 'gal pa da du ra'i khu bas bsad te//'byung po'i nyi ma la dur khrog kyi sol bas shing leb bo//rtsig ngos la 'byung po'i mtshan mo gang snun pa der ltung bar 'gyuro//
101a6(rgyud): sang gi ta'i smyug gus sngags pas cho ga snga ma bzhin du byas la//thal ba la mngon par sngags te/gang la snun pa de sha zas bzung bar 'gyur ro//dur khrod kyi sol bas shing leb ba rtsig ngos
101a7(rgyud): la 'byung bo'i mtshan mo gang gi snying kar ming bris pa de gdon chen pos bzung bar 'gyur ro//sgrub bya'i gzugs byas la//de'i snying khar 'phrul 'khor bcug ste//zhag bdun gyi bar du nang du bzhag
101a8(rgyud): nas /a ta mu ta'i lcug ma la lan bdun sngags te//de la lan bdun nas zhag bdun gyi bar du mthar rlung gi shugs kyis 'ong bar 'gyuro//'khor lo de ni bye brag tu dur khro kyi ris la po nya'i kha
101b1(rgyud): dog bye brag du gyur pa bris la las kyi pho nya dang lan cig bsgrub par bya'o//ka ra bi ri'i lcug ma khru phyed dang gnyis kyi tshad dang ldan pa la gang la mngon par 'tshogs te//sngags bris nas /ro
101b2(rgyud): bsreg pa'i me 'bar ba la gtor te ji srid ma tshig la myur du blangs la//rig pa'i rgyal po lan bdun bzlas la /gang zhig dgran de klang la skor na//de'i mod la rlung gi dkyil 'khor
101b3(rgyud): gyi shugs kyis 'ong bar 'gyur ro//skyes pa'i ri'i kham phor la//ba gla dang/yung ba'i khu bas gang gi ming bris la//de mdo la bcings par 'gyur ro//dur khrod kyi ras sam zla mtshan gyi
101b4(rgyud): ras la shing a sho ka'i smyug gus klu rnams spangs pa'i 'khor lo 'di bris la//de'i dbus su sngags pas cho ga bzhin yi ge dang spel ba ni//mkha' 'gro ma'i khyad par thas cad dang lus sprul po thaMs
101b5(rgyud): cad dang bcings par 'gyur//thod pa la skyes pa'i khrag gis gang gi ming bris la //sngags pas khro ba dang bcas pas sngags bzlas na //de'i khrag khugs par byed la //yid zhi ba
101b6(rgyud): ldan pas zhi ba'i sngags bzlas na //slar stong par 'gyur ro//'khor lo de nyid zla bas brgyan cing gsal bar go ro tsa nas gro ga la skar ma rgyal gyi nyin par bris te/bud med gang gis rtag
101b7(rgyud): tu bcangs na /rig pa'i rgyal mo nyid de'i lhums su 'jug par 'gyur ro//bud med dang zhi ba'i 'dus pas kun du spyod pa'i mi'i mchog de'i lag 'thil na yod pa'i dpal gang gis kyang 'phrog par mi
101b8(rgyud): nus so//rnam pa de lta bu'i cho ga dang ldan pas ni/dngos grub thams cad kyi 'bras bu ster bar 'gyur ro//bcom ldan 'das 'di ni rtul shugs dang /spyod pa dang /spyod smyung ba dang /cho ga med par yang
102a1(rgyud): @#//cho ga bzhib mo'i sbyor ba'i tshul 'di nyid kyis las thaMs cad ji ltar 'dod pa bzhin byed do//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus bsaM par /zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dam pa ste//'od zer tshogs
102a2(rgyud): ni gsal ba'i mdangs/'khor lo'i phreng bas rnam par klubs//de dbus 'khor lo rtsibs brgyad dang /pad ma'i phreng bas shin du mdzes //slar yang 'khor lo bri bar bya//rdo rje phreng bar 
102a3(rgyud): rnam par 'phro//sku gsuMng thugs kyi rdo rje'i //dkyil 'khor lta bu yin/nam mkha' thams cad g.yog byed pa //rang gi lus las spro bar bya//dkar dang dmar dang sngo ba ste//kha dog mchog
102a4(rgyud): gi bye brag bya//bya ba'i cho ga 'di yis ni /dpa' bo'i dbang phyug de bzhin bri /dbus su bsgrub par bya ba'i ming //ci ltar 'dod bzhin bsgrub par bya//phyi rol rdo rje'i phreng ba ni //'od zer 'bar
102a5(rgyud): ba'i phreng 'khrug bri//'di ni srung ba chen po ste//thaMs cad bsrung ba bla na med //rims dang nad dang dug dang ni//'kho' 'gro ma'i 'tshe ba dang //gdon dang bdud dang log 'dren dang /
102a6(rgyud): gang yang lus sprul la sogs pa//grib ma yang ni mi mthong na//de'i lus la lta ci smos //zla gzugs lta bu'i yi ge lta /phyogs bcur rnam par dbye bar byed //dkar po yis ni 
102a7(rgyud): zhi 'gyur te//nag pos skad cig dag gis bsod//dmar mo po yis ni de dbang byed//de nyid kyis ni nges par 'gugs //ser pos nges par 'ching 'gyur te//dmag gi dpung ni 'ching
102a8(rgyud): bar 'gyur //zhes bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs te//las thams cad 'byung ba zhes ba'i bsrung ba'i 'khor lo'o//ka'i 'khor lo khyad par ni /de nas yang dag bshad bya ba /las
102b1(rgyud): kyi cho ga mthong ba yis//sems can kun gyi don byed pa'o//nam mkha' dbings dbus gnas par zla ba'i dkyil 'khor lta bu yi//dkyil 'khor lta bur bsaM//de dbus pad ma bri bar bya//drug cu 'dab mas rnaM par
102b2(rgyud): rgyan//de bus pad chen 'bad ma rgyad//rdo rje rtse mo lnga pa yi//mchog//'od zer dkar pos shin tu mdzes//sgrub pos 'khor lo'i phyi rol bri//pad ma'i lte bar swAhA bri//dngos grub thams cad rab ster
102b3(rgyud): ma mo'i khyim ni nyams dga' bar//tsan dan bzang pos byugs nas ni//'bol zhing shin tu 'dzaM pa yi//stan las skyes nas phyag rgyas bcings//sngags pas bsgrub bya//mngon phyogs te//
102b4(rgyud): brgya rtsa brgyad du zlas par bya//yan lag rus pa rgyan bsam zhing //gcer bur gyur pas rang gi bri//yi dgas dogs si/ro yi ni//buM pa skam po dag la yang //yi dgas sen rtsi khrag
102b5(rgyud): dang ni//de bzhin tsan dan dmar pos bri//'khor lo de'i snying khar gzhug//ji ltar 'dod bzhin thun gsuM du//blo ldan the cung skyod nas ni//bram ze'i bsod pa nyi bdun 'grub//tsan dan dmar po'i rdul las
102b6(rgyud): ni//sgrub par bya ba'i gzugs byas la//tsha ba gsuM gyi khus sbyar nas//sbra tshil gyis ni byug par bya//zangs ma'i khyab kyis skye nas phug//seng ldeng 'dab ma du med la//blo dang ldan pas rin bdun
102b7(rgyud): sro//sgrub po yis ni brgya bzlas na//rgyal po blon po 'dun na 'don//btsun mo'i 'khor dang bcas pa ni//myur du 'gugs par byed pa yin//grog yi ni 'dab ma la//ro'i sen rtsi ro rtsi nas//mi'i bdag
102b8(rgyud): po 'khor bcas pa//ming dang spel te bri bar bya//mngon par phyogs te zlos bzhin du//seng ldeng dang ni dur khrod me//ji srid spra tshil ma zhu bar//me'i steng du bdug par bya//bsgrub bya mngon par
103a1(rgyud): @#//bdugs tsam gyis //lha dang lha min 'od gyur na//sa stengs skye bo smos ci dgos//yi dgas sen rtsi skyes pa yi//khrag dang ge'ang bcas pa yis //bsgrub bya'i mngi spel gro gar bri//bsgrub par bya ba'i rkang rjes
103a2(rgyud): sa//tsan dan dmar po nyid dang ni//dur khrod zas lhag thal ba rnams /bsgrub pa pos ni gcig sdus nas//seng ldeng me ni du med la /rtul de lag pa gnyis kyis ni /gnyis nas sbyin sreg byas pa yis //bal
103a3(rgyud): ba de ni bzung nas su//bsgrub par bya ba'i gzugs byas te//snying kar 'phrul 'khor bcug nas ni//zlos bzhin du ni me gdungs na//rgyal zhing sems dang bral bar 'gyur //rmongs shing rab tu myos nas ni//
103a4(rgyud): rlung gi shugs kyis 'ong bar 'gyur //cho ga bya ba 'di yis ni //yi ge nyi shu rtsa gnyis gzhed //lha mo sngags chen 'di dag ni//mkha'agro ma'i snying 'joms yin//de nas de bzhin gshegs kun gyis//yid
103a5(rgyud): 'ong zhi ba skyed pa mo //ka'i 'khor lo bshad par bya//mkha' dbyings dbus 'gyur bsam pa ni //zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dam pa ste//rdo rje rtse gsuM kha bzhi pa der ni de nas bra bar bya//de dbus pad
103a6(rgyud): ma 'dab brgyad pa//shin tu mdzes par bris nasu//be ro tsa na'i rig pa che//sems can kun skyed sdug pa mo//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs //nad rnams 
103a7(rgyud): thaMs cad med par byed pa zhes bya ba'i bsrung ba'i 'khor lo'i sbyor ba'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das kun du mkhyen pa'i ye shes mngon par shes pa yis//'khor lo zhes bka' stsal pa//gsar lte bar ldan 
103a8(rgyud): pa yi//pad ma 'dab ma drug pa bri//zla ba bzang pos nye bar brgyan//rdo rje phreng ba rnaM par klubs//de'i phyir ni rol 'khor lo ste//ston ka'i zla ltar dri ma med//yi ge'i mchog ni rnaM pa bdun//lha mo chen mo phan
103b1(rgyud): pa gcig//bdud thams cad kyi las 'joms pa'i 'khor lo'i rgyal po zhes bya'o//tsan dan dkar dang ro tsa nas //gro ga la ni bris nasu/'khor lo lag pa g.yon par bcad//'khor lo 'di ni lha mo che//las

103b3(rgyud): par grol//'gyur nyid du ni the tsom med//lag pa g.yon par 'khor lo 'di //zla ba'i dbus su rnam bkod na//ston ka'i ni zla 'drar 'gyur //'khor lo 'di ni lha mtsho//las kyi 'od kyis rab brgyan pa//
103b4(rgyud): las kyi cho ga mthong ba yis//bya ba thams cad byed par 'gyur//dur khrod ras saM thod pa 'am//ar dkar yi ni 'dab ma la //sngar stan pa'i sol ba la //skyes pa'i khrag ni snag tsas bri//sgrub
103b5(rgyud): bya'i ming smyug gus bri//nyi ma'i dbus 'gyur khro sems kyis//gzugs rnyan byas la bsgrub bya'i//dbus su de ni bcug nasu//skye tse yis ni byugs nas ni//yang dang yang du bsre bar bya//sngags
103b6(rgyud): kyi rgyal pos bskul ba yin//bya ba thams cad byed pa 'gyur//'di ni nad rnams thaMs cad ni//'phel ba dang de bzhin skyong byed pa'o//rims la yi dgas snying kar bri //dga' ra yidag klad par ste//rjed
103b7(rgyud): byed rlung dang srin la bri//gzer bar bya la mdung la ste//dug dang mdze nad lto 'phye la gdon la gdon gyi mtshan mar bri//mdor bsdus nas ni 'khor lo bkod//shel dang zla bA 'od 'dra ba'i //yi ge brtun rtsi rgyun
103b8(rgyud): 'bab pa'i//ston ka'i zer lta bur ni //bsam gtan bsams pa tsam gyis ni//'chi ba nges par slong bar 'gyur//sbrul chen brgya yis nas ni//'chi ba 'ang myur du slong bar byed//dug gi rgyal bar 'gyur
104a1(rgyud): @#//bdag/stan pa tsam gyis slong bar 'gyur//rims dang gdon dang garjed byed dang //lag pa mthong bas rlag par 'gyur //grib ma la ni mi 'gom na//de'i lus la lta ci smos//zangs ma yi ni 'dab
104a2(rgyud): mal //lag pa g.yon pas 'khor lo 'di /bab la dang ni yung bas //de nas chu'i nang cug la//ci ltar 'byung zhing 'bad de mchod//'tshams kyi dus su mnyam bar ni //de bzhin du ni sngags bzlas pas//
104a3(rgyud): chu'i nang nas bton nasu //'o mas dkrus nas rgya mtshor bcug|g.yul du khu tshur bcangs nasu //rgyan rnams kun gyis rab brgyan te//yid la rtag tu bzlos byed na//ci srid khu tshur ma krol bar //myur du
104a4(rgyud): thos cad rengs 'gyur te//dgra bo lag te lag na ni//mtshon cha thogs te char na bzang /ci srid khu tshur ma krol bar //mtshon cha gtong bar mi nuso//yi ge bdun dang rnal 'byor ma/drug gi yi ge 'di dag ni//'khor
104a5(rgyud): lo drug la gnas par ni//lag pa'i dbus su bkod nasu //bsgrub bya'i rkang rjes rtul dag ni//gcig tu byas nas rtag par bya/lan brgyar zlas te rab sgoms na//sa bdag skye bo nor dang

114a1(rgyud): //ti ni hi las byung //mdog sngon lag pa bzhi pa ste//kha traM kha'i thod pa dang //bshor dang de bzhin Da ma ru //bam las byung ba'i tshangs pa mo//mdog dkar lag pa bzhi pa ste//pad ma dang thod pa dang

120b8(rgyud): brgyad rgyun ni//zla ba dag ni brgyad dang ni//zhag nyi ma'i grangasu ni//de'i dus ni bshad pa yin//gal te nyin zhag cu dgu na//dri yi gnod pa rgyu ba dag//ci srid mtsho bar bshad par bya//

132a7(rgyud): dang rab dman dman pa can//gnod sbyin lha min sgrub pa po//klu dang byol song gsang ba pa//lto 'phye dang ni mi 'am ci//mi dang yid btags mkha' 'gro ma//dmyal ba la sogs gang 'gyur pa//gsal ba'i
132a8(rgyud): gzugs su mngon brjod pa//phra mo sgong nga las byung dang //chu dang me dang rlung dang ni//de bzhin du ni sa'i khams//brtan pa la sogs thams cad dang //rtswa dang 'dab ma 'phra ba dang //sa dang rdo dang brag
132b1(rgyud): la sogs//thams cad ye shes byin rlabs pas//thams cad snang ste gzhan du na //ye shes med par skye mi 'gyur //skye ba bye ba stong phrag tu //sangs rgyas ye shes kyis skul bas //de bas rnal 'byor stan
132b2(rgyud): chos la//'bad de dad pa skyed par 'gyur//gar gyi gnas dang mtshungs pa yi//phyi rol stan chos sun 'byin pa //rnal 'byor stan chos rjes 'grengs nas//spyod dang 'gro ba'i 'bras bu thob//snying po las kyang
132b3(rgyud): snying po'i mchog/rnal 'byor bstan chos mtshon par bya//mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba'i//rdo rje mkha' 'gro bde ba'i mchog/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs //bcom ldan rdo rje can
132b4(rgyud): gyis gsungs//rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro las //rdo rje sems dpa'i rnam par 'phrul pa las //'byung ba'i rim par phye ba ste//sum bcu pa'o////
132b5(rgyud): de nas yang dag bshad bya ba //rdo rje dril bu'i mtshan nyid de rdo rje sems sogs lha rnams su//rdo rje blos ni shes par bya //zhi dang rgyas dang mngon spyod kyi//rdo rje dag ni thams cad kyang
132b6(rgyud): gser la sogs pa legs par gdungs//rdo rje kun gyis kun tu brgyan//cha gsum dag ni gzung nas su//cha brgyad skye rags legs par brgyan//phyogs dang phyogs mtshams brgyad dang //dbu ma
132b7(rgyud): gnyis dang sangs rgyas dgu//gtso bo dgus ni lha nyid de//lha dgu yis ni byin gyis brlabs//chu srin las ni byung ba yi//rtse mor pad ma zla ba gnas//rtse dgu zhes ni bya ba yi//ye shes rdo rje 'gro ba sgrol//
132b8(rgyud): cha gsum gyi tshad dag la //dbu ma cung zad ring yang bzung //'di ni thun mong tshad yin no//rtse mo kun gyi dbu ma zlum//phyogs sogs rtse mo gdung bas gnas//dbus ni zur brgyad dag tu bya //dbus
133a1(rgyud): @#//kyi cha'i tshad ldan pa//gnas pa lta ba zhes bya'o//rin chen pad ma ra ga ltar /de yang cung zad zur brgyad bya //steng dang 'og dang dbus dag ni//so sor cha ni gsum gsum 'dod//
133a2(rgyud): steng gi pad ma zla ba ni//drug cha kun nas zlum por bya//de dag la ni seng ge'i sna//nas phyed tshad mtho bar bya//mche bi yi ni mthar gnas pa//de ni drug cha zhes byar grags//cha ni nyi shu rtsa bdun ste//so so
133a3(rgyud): ru ni dgu dgur 'dod//cha gsum bla ma'i bi mtha' ma ru//steng gi cha gnyis spangs nas ni//dbus kyi mthar yang de bzhin no//'dab ma la ni gnas pa yi//chu srin cha ni gnyid yin te//de'i phyed du skye rags
133a4(rgyud): 'dod//rdo rje kun la blo ldan pas//chu dang zheng ni mnyam por sbyor//de nas yang dag bshad par bya//dam tshig rdo rje'i mtshan nyid de//gser la sogs pa'i cha brdungs pa//cha la sog+sa
133a5(rgyud): pa sngon du ni/ji ltar bstan pa bzhin bya/pad ma zla ba la gnas pa'i//chu srin gyi ni mche ba'i gzugs//'og gi chu srin las byung ba//chu zheng ji ltar bstan pa yi//rtse mo ye
133a6(rgyud): shes rang bzhin 'byung //kun gyi dbus su rnam snang ste//shar phyogs su mi skyod pa//lho ru rin chen 'byung gnas te//'od dpag med pa nub du 'god//byang du don yod grub pa ste//rtse bzhi la sog+sa
133a7(rgyud): lha yin no//pad ma byang chub yan lag brgyad//sa la ci ltar dgod par bya//phyag na pad ma byams pa dag/mkha' snying kun du bzang mo dang //gnod sbyin bdag dang 'jam pa'i dpal//sa snying sgrib sel

133b1(rgyud): gnas su sbyar bar bya//rdo rje lcags kyu zhags pa dang //rdo rje lcags sgrog dril bu nyid //lha mo dag ni bzhi po 'di//phyogs kyi pad mar yang dag gnas //'tshams ni bzhi po dag tu yang //slar yang de
133b2(rgyud): bzhin lha mo bzhin //bskul byed rin chen sgron ma dang //de bzhin rdo rje lcags sgrog dang //de bzhin gzhan mi thub ma //pad ma'i 'dab ma kun la bkod//rtse mo dag ni rnam lnga la//mkha' 'gro ma ni lnga

133b4(rgyud): rdo rje rtse gsum ste//rtse mo dag ni gdengs por bya//de bzhin rtse mo gcig pa ni //klu rnams skrag par byed pa'o//khro bo rdo rje gdengs po che //lhag ma rnams ni 'dul byed pa'o//zhi la zhi ba'i
133b5(rgyud): lha nyid de //rig sa kyi rim pa cho gas dbye//las kyi cho ga gso ba yis//bya ba thams cad byed par 'gyur //de nas gzhan yang bshad bya ba//gsang ba rdo rje'i mtshan nyid do//cha lnga'i
133b6(rgyud): tshad dang ldan pa ni//spyi gtsug rdo rjer zhes byar grags //sna cha cha drug tshad du bya //mgul do cha ni bzhi pa ste//snying kar cha lnga pa ni bzung //gsang ba dang lag par yang //cha drug la ni bzung bya ste//ji
133b7(rgyud): ltar mdzes par rtag par bya//rdo rje'i brgyan gyis brgyan nas ni//rnal 'byor pa'i ye shes 'phel//cho ga'i bya ba 'di nyid kyis //rnal 'byor rig par rtag tu sgom//de nas dril bu'i mtshan nyid kyi//chu zheng
133b8(rgyud): la sogs bshad par bya //rin chen sna tshogs 'bar ba dang //rdo rje skye rags phreng gnyis kyis//shel sgong dri ma med pa ltar //dra ba dra ba phyed par brgyan //cha gsum mtshan nyid ldan pa la//rdo rje
134a1(rgyud): //lasogs mtshan mas brgyan //dbug gi khog pa zab pa dang /thams cad dag byed bcud kyis gnas //de nas pad ma rin chen dang //ud pal ku mu ta yis brgyan //phyogs dang
134a2(rgyud): phyogs mtshams cha brgyad du //lha mo lasogs rgyang du grags //de'i steng du lha mo ni //sa tshon brgyad las bsam par bya //shar phyogs su ni sgrol ma ste//de bzhin byang du gos kar mo//nub du pad ma 
134a3(rgyud): ming can de /lho ru sangs rgyas spyan zhes bya //mdog mdzes sbrang rtsi yid 'ong ma//de bzhin rin chen 'khyil ba mo//nam mkha'i phyogs kyi dbus su ni//gzugs rdzogs bskor bas yang dag gnas //
134a4(rgyud): pad ma rgyas pa lta bu'i dbyibs //gzugs med srid pa'i phyogs su ni //ye shes bdud rtsi'i bum pa 'dra//shes rab bdud rtsi'i gnas yin te//de'i dbus kyi bstan la ni //steng du 
134a5(rgyud): pad ma rdo rje ni//rtse mo dgu pa bsam par bya//dbus kyi dbig pa ye shes ni //thams cad spro zhing sdu ba mo//oM lhar mchog gi rtsol ba 'grub //bya ba thams cad sgrub pa'o//ye shes bdud rtsi
134a6(rgyud): sgrub pa'o//ye shes bdud rtsa'i dril bu ni //rtse mo dgu pa de bzhin no//sor mo bcu gnyis tshad du ni//rdo rje phreng bas skye rags byas//pad ma mchog gi 'dab ma la //dra ba dra ba phyed pas brgyan//sngon bzhin mtshan nyid
134a7(rgyud): ldan pa la//lha mo yi ni mtshan ma mdzes //shar gyi phyogs su pad ma ni//kha dog dkar pos mdzes par bya//gshin rje pad ma sngon dmar ste//nub kyi phyogs su pad ma dmar //byang du pad ma la ni //bdud rtsi
134a8(rgyud): gnas kyi dbus su ni//lha mo yi ni zhal cig bri//shes rab pha rol phyin lha mo//gzugs mdzes bskor bas yang dag gnas//steng gi pad ma rdo rje ni//snga ma bzhin du bsam par bya//dbus kyi ye shes dbyig pa ni//
134b1(rgyud): zur brgyad pa ni mdzes par bya//dril bu 'di ni lha mo che//tshe dang nad med phun sum tshogs//de nas dril bu'i dril bu ste//rdo rje phreng ba'i skye rags ldan//de bzhin du ni shes rab dang //bdud rtsi'i bum par ldan
134b2(rgyud): mtshan nyid thams cad yongs su rdzogs//dra ba dra ba phyed pas spras//sor dgu n-i yangs pa ste//kha dog sor lnga'i tshad du bya//de las cung zad lhag pa min//rtse lnga pa'i rtse dgu pa//skal pa bzang po thams cad
134b3(rgyud): dang /dbang phyug dang ni sngags rnams kyi//'bras bu thams cad rab ster cing //nam mkha' 'gro'-i stobs dang rdzu 'phrul ster//de bzhin phun tshogs thams cad dang //de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi//
134b4(rgyud): dngos grub mchog ni bsal bar byed//da ni de bzhin gshegs pa yi//dril bu'i mtshan nyid bshad par bya//khar ba las byung sgra snyan byed//khong stong de ni bum par ldan//rdo rje rin chen pad ma
134b5(rgyud): dang ral gri la sogs pad ma can//dra ba dra ba phyed pa sa spras chu srin las ni byung ba yi//ye shes lnga n-i rnam dag pas//rtse mo lnga dang ldan pa ste//skye rags gnyis dang gnyis dbus ni//
134b6(rgyud): 'khor lo'-i phreng ba gnyis dang ldan//de bzhin skye rags gnyis dbus su//pad ma la ni pad phreng gnyis//rin chen la ni rin po che//de bzhin skye rags ldan par bya//don yod grub pa zhes bya ba//skye rags
134b7(rgyud): gnyis dbus ral gris brgyan//kun ldan shes rab gdong ldan pas//dpa' bo kun gyis rgyal bar 'gyur//kun la'ang mkhar ba la 'am yang na ni//lcags gsum las ni dril bu ste//cha gsum la ni
134b8(rgyud): ldan par bya//rdo rje rigs las la ba glang gdong //skye rags rdo rje phreng bas skor//bum pa'i steng du gnas pa yi//kha khyer cung zad gru bzhir bya//ba lang gdong ni myur ba ru//nor dang 'bru rnams 'phel bar byed/
135a1(rgyud): //nad med stobs ni rtag ster zhing //sdug bsngal thams cad 'phrog par byed //sngar bstan rjes su 'brangs nas ni//rdo rje'i mtshan nyid mtshon par bya//rin chen dbang sogs thod pa dang //rang rang
135a2(rgyud): mtshan ma shes par bya//sor brgyad nas ni bcu drug gam //nyi shu las ni lhag mi bya//ji ltar nus shing rjes su 'thun //sgrub pa'i dbang pos de dag bya//kha yang cho ga'i rim pa yis //sor mo lnga las zhan pa spang //
135a3(rgyud): lha mo 'di ni dril bu yi//sgrubs thabs rgya cher bstan pa ste//thun mong gi ni rgyud kun las //dpa' bo rnams kyis sngags pa yin//mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba'i//rdo rje mkha' bde ba'i mchog//rdo rje sems
135a4(rgyud): dpa' de bzhin gshegs//bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs//rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro las//rdo rje dang dril bu'i mtshan nyid dang //tshad kyi cho ga'i rim par phye ba'i

135a6(rgyud): sbyin//sngags kyi rgyud kyi sbyor ba shes//rnal 'byor bzo dang dus la mkhas//thams cad du ni 'jam por smra//sems can kun la bu bzhin lta//rtag tu rtong la dga' ba dang //bsam gtan sbyor la mngon par rtson
135a7(rgyud): snying rje phan pa'i sems kyis ni//bden par smri zhing 'tshe mi byed//rtag tu mnyam pa'i sems kyis ni//sems can rnams kyi skyo ba ste//sems can bsam pa'i bye brag mkhas//mgon med rnams la snying ltar rtse
135a8(rgyud): mdza' sdug gis ni legs par lta//sngags kyi tshul la shin du goms//dam tshig spyod la rab tu gus//phyag rgya gda'i sbyor ba rig/rnal 'byor ma rnams dam tshig la//gda'i sbyor ba ston pa dang //gnyis med
135b1(rgyud): sbyar ba mi phyed gnas//sngags kyi 'khor lo spyod la gus//dbang skur don gyi yon tan 'byung //gnas sogs mthong ba'i ngang tshul can//de nyid bcu ni yongs su shes//'di ni slob dpon so zhes byar brjod//

135b3(rgyud): sangs rgyas te//'jig rten pa ni zhabs kyis mnan//'od zer gnod gsang la sogs//de ltar yon tan rang bzhin lus//rnal 'byor can gyis rtag tu lta//tsi lta'i mtshan nyid dang ldan pa//ye shes can gyis
135b4(rgyud): mtshan shes nas//de lta'i bla ma 'bad de ste//gang yang khro dang sgyu can dang //ngag ni shin du rtsub pa dang //yon tan dag gis chog shes pas//des ni bla ma bsten mi bya//gzhan yang slob ma'i

135b7(rgyud): dpon gyi ni gsang ba dang //rnal 'byor ma dang lha'i sbyor ba dang //lha'i dam tshig spyod pa dang //gtor ma la sogs gsang sbyor ba'i//snyen ma dag tu gang byed pa//skrag pa kun dang bcas bzhin du//mnar med par
135b8(rgyud): ni ltung bar 'gyur//dpon dpon gyi dpon ni mal bstan dang //lham dang gdugs na bza' sogs//nye ba longs spyod thams cad dang //grib ma 'gom par mi bya zhing //co dri bar ni mi bya zhing /gal te byas na dus
136a1(rgyud): @#//kun du//de ni kun du gzir 'gyur zhing //don nyams dpal las ring du 'gyur//bdud dang sdang ba gzir 'gyur te//mchod rten dang gshig dang sdig mtshungs pas//de bas dri ba ma 'gom mi bya//slob

137b1(rgyud): mahi swahA/hUM bo shad de hUM hUM|ho phaT hUM phaT/oM hoM hiM//hUM hUM hoM hoM/oM baM haM yaM /hriM maM /hriM hUM hUM hUM phaT phaT /oM sarba b+hud da badzra pa ni ye /badzra da ki ni swAhA//oM su b+ha ni sum b+ha ni hUM hUM phaT/g+hi na g+hi na

219a2(rgyud): so ni ston pa la//de la le ni bstan par bya//gang zhi ga mthu ni ston byed pa//de la o skos ston par bya//gang zhig mgrin pa ston byed pa//lto ba de la stan par bya//gang zhig lag pa 
219a3(rgyud): ston byed pa//de la dpung pa bstan par bya//gang zhigi rkyad pa ston byed pa//de la rgyab ni bstan par bya//gang gzhig nu ma ston pa la//de la kos sgo bstan par bya//gang zhi

219b1(rgyud): gnyis ni bstan pa yis//legs 'ongs lan ni smra ba yin//'di ni rnal 'byor ma kun gyi//yan lag phyag rgya rab tu brgyan//'dzub mor gnyis kug mig gi brgya//sngags

219b3(rgyud): byed pa//de la lce ni bstan par bya//gang zhigapa tis ston pa//de la rtse gsum bstan par bya//gang zhigaspyi bo ston byed pa//de la mtshams kyi mtha' stan bya//gang zhig
219b4(rgyud): sa ni ston byed pa//de la kha ni bstan par bya//gang gi khro gnyer ston byed pa//de la 'tshams ni bstan par bya//gang cig so ni ston byed pa//de lamachu ni stan par bya//gang zhig
219b5(rgyud): ma gin pa ston byed pa//de la kha na bstan par bya//gang zhin dpral ba ston byed pa//de la lto ba bstan par bya//'di ni rnal 'byor ma kun gyi//rims bzhin yan lag phyag rgya yin

220a1(rgyud): /rtag tu gzhan gyi srin ma mchog/sna tshogs myur du shes bya ba/de bzhin mkha' 'gro las byung ba'o//rnam 'gyur 'khyil bar shes bya ste/ri mo gsum pa yongs su zlum /'kha'

220b1(rgyud): shi ro n-i bdun no//ka ru ni lte ba'o/pa ra ni skra ba'o/skro ma ni rna ba'o/a mri ta s+tha ni srubs pa'o//sa ga ma ni mi'o/ta la ka'i ni mkha' 'gro ma'o/na ra ka ni dkyil 'khor ro/a ma ka ni dur

224a5(rgyud): gsum pa yis ni khyad par dbye//de nas sum bcu gnyis pa blang//lnga pa yi ni khyab par bya//de nas nyi shu lnga pa bzung //nyi shu bzhi pa dag dang sbyar //
224a6(rgyud): sum bcu rtsa gsum yang dag blang //ri'u mig bcwa lnga pa yis ni ldan//lnga pa dag dang yang dag bcas//mdzes pa'i mchog ni yin par 'dod//ri'u min ni bcu dgu pa//me'i bu dang
